````math
martingales and is exactly matched with the Markovian structure of our
problem. We first provide a user-friendly version of the Freedman
inequality introduced in ([Li et al., 2023](#bib.bib3), Section C).

###### Theorem 4 (Freedman’s inequality)

Consider a filtration
$`\mathcal{F}_{0}\subset\mathcal{F}_{1}\subset\mathcal{F}_{2}\subset\cdots`$,
and let $`\mathbb{E}_{k}`$ stand for the expectation conditioned on
$`\mathcal{F}_{k}`$. Suppose that
$`Y_{n}=\sum_{k=1}^{n}X_{k}\in\mathbb{R}`$, where $`\{X_{k}\}`$ is a
real-valued scalar sequence obeying

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\left|X_{k}\right|\leq R\qquad\text{and}\qquad\mathbb{E}_{k-1}\big[X_{k}\big]=0\quad\quad\quad\text{for all }k\geq 1
``` |  |

for some quantity $`R<\infty`$. We also define

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
F_{n}\coloneqq\sum_{k=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{k-1}\left[X_{k}^{2}\right].
``` |  |

In addition, suppose that $`F_{n}\leq\psi^{2}`$ holds deterministically
for some given quantity $`\psi^{2}<\infty`$. Then for any positive
integer $`m\geq 1`$, with probability at least $`1-\delta`$ one has

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\left|Y_{n}\right|\leq\sqrt{8\max\Big\{F_{n},\frac{\psi^{2}}{2^{m}}\Big\}\log\frac{2m}{\delta}}+\frac{4}{3}R\log\frac{2m}{\delta}.
``` |  | (40) |

### B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality

Before diving into the subsequent proof, we first introduce two lemmas
based on Friedman’s inequality. Our first result is concerned with a
martingale concentration bound as follows.

###### Lemma 7

Let
$`\big\{F_{h}^{k}\in\mathbb{R}^{S}\mid 1\leq k\leq K,1\leq h\leq H+1\big\}`$
and
$`\big\{G_{h}^{i}(s,a,b,N)\in\mathbb{R}\mid 1\leq k\leq K,1\leq h\leq H+1\big\}`$
be the collections of vectors and scalars, respectively, and suppose
that they obey the following properties:

- •
  $`F_{h}^{k}`$ is fully determined by the samples collected up to the
  end of the $`(h-1)`$-th step of the $`i`$-th episode;
- •
  $`\|F_{h}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{\mathrm{f}}`$;
- •
  $`G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)`$ is fully determined by the samples collected up
  to the end of the $`(h-1)`$-th step of the $`i`$-th episode, and a
  given positive integer $`N\in[K]`$;
- •
  $`0\leq G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\leq C_{\mathrm{g}}`$;
- •
  $`0\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}G_{h}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b,N)\leq 2`$.

In addition, for $`1\leq k\leq K`$, consider the following sequence

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle X_{k}(s,a,b,h,N)`$ | $`\displaystyle\coloneqq G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\big(P_{h}^{k}\!-\!P_{h,s,a,b}\big)F_{h+1}^{k}\mathbbm{1}\big\{(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=(s,a,b)\big\},`$ |  | (41) |

with $`P_{h}^{k}`$ defined in
([15](#A1.E15 "In 2nd item ‣ A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Consider any $`\delta\in(0,1)`$. Then with probability at least
$`1-\delta`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{i=1}^{k}X_{k}(s,a,b,h,N)\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}u_{h}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b,N)\mathsf{Var}_{h,s,a,b}\big(W_{h+1}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}\big)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad+\left(C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}+\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathrm{g}}}{N}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\right)\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  | (42) |

holds simultaneously for all
$`(k,h,s,a,b,N)\in[K]\times[H]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[K]`$.

###### Proof B.1

On a non-ambiguous basis, we will abbreviate $`X_{k}(s,a,b,h,N)`$ as
$`X_{i}`$ throughout the proof of this lemma. The main idea of our proof
is to control the $`\sum_{i=1}^{k}X_{i}`$ term by Freedman’s inequality
(see  Theorem
 [4](#Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4 (Freedman’s inequality) ‣ B.1 A user-friendly version of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

Consider any given
$`(k,h,s,a,b,N)\in[K]\times[H]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[K]`$.
It can be easily verified that

|     |                                       |     |
|-----|---------------------------------------|-----|
|     |
       ``` math
       \mathbb{E}_{k-1}\left[X_{k}\right]=0,
       ```                                    |     |

where $`\mathbb{E}_{k-1}`$ denotes the expectation conditioned on
everything happening up to the end of the $`(h-1)`$-th step of the
$`k`$-th episode. Furthermore, under the assumptions
$`\|F^{k}_{h+1}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{\mathrm{f}}`$,
$`0\leq G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\leq C_{\mathrm{g}}`$, and the basic facts
$`\big\|P^{k}_{h}\big\|_{1}=\big\|P_{h,s,a,b}\big\|_{1}=1`$, we have the
following crude bound:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big|X_{i}\big|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\Big|\big(P^{k}_{h}-P_{h,s,a,b}\big)F^{k}_{h+1}\Big|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\Big(\big\|P^{k}_{h}\big\|_{1}+\big\|P_{h,s,a,b}\big\|_{1}\Big)\big\|F^{k}_{h+1}\big\|_{\infty}\leq 2C_{\mathrm{f}}C_{\mathrm{g}}.`$ |  | (43) |

Under the definition of the variance parameter in
([16](#A1.E16 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we obtain

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{k}\mathbb{E}_{k-1}\left[\big|X_{k}\big|^{2}\right]`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{k}\!\!\big(G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\big)^{2}\mathbbm{1}\big\{(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\!\!=\!\!(s,a,b)\big\}\mathbb{E}_{k-1}\left[\big|(P_{h}^{k}\!-\!P_{h,s,a,b})F_{h+1}^{k}\big|^{2}\right]`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}\big(G_{h}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b,N)\big)^{2}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s,a,b}\big(F_{h+1}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{g}}\Bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}G_{h}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b,N)\Bigg)\big\|F_{h+1}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}\big\|_{\infty}^{2}\leq 2C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}^{2},`$ |  | (44) |

where the inequalities hold true due to the assumptions
$`\|F_{h}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{\mathrm{f}}`$,
$`0\leq G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\leq C_{\mathrm{g}}`$, and
$`0\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}G_{h}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b,N)\leq 2`$.

Armed with
([43](#A2.E43 "In Proof B.1 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([44](#A2.E44 "In Proof B.1 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we can invoke
Theorem [4](#Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4 (Freedman’s inequality) ‣ B.1 A user-friendly version of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
(with $`m=\lceil\log_{2}N\rceil`$) and take the union bound over all
$`(k,h,s,a,N)\in[K]\times[H]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[K]`$
to show that: with probability at least $`1-\delta`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{k=1}^{k}X_{k}\bigg|\lesssim C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\log\frac{SABT^{2}\log N_{h}^{k}}{\delta}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{\max\Bigg\{C_{\mathrm{g}}\!\!\!\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}\!\!\!u_{h}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b,N)\mathsf{Var}_{h,s,a}\big(W_{h+1}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}\big),\frac{C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}}{N}\Bigg\}\log\frac{SABT^{2}\log N}{\delta}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}\!\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}u_{h}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b,N)\mathsf{Var}_{h,s,a}\big(W_{h+1}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}\big)}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\left(C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}+\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathrm{g}}}{N}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\right)\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |

holds simultaneously for all
$`(k,h,s,a,b,N)\in[K]\times[H]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[K]`$.

###### Lemma 8

Let
$`\big\{N(s,a,b,h)\in[K]\mid(s,a,b,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[H]\big\}`$
be a collection of positive integers, and let
$`\{c_{h}:0\leq c_{h}\leq e,h\in[H]\}`$ be a collection of fixed and
bounded universal constants. Moreover, let
$`\big\{F_{h}^{k}\in\mathbb{R}^{S}\mid 1\leq k\leq K,1\leq h\leq H+1\big\}`$
and
$`\big\{G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\in\mathbb{R}\mid 1\leq i\leq K,1\leq h\leq H+1\big\}`$
represent respectively a collection of random vectors and scalars, which
obey the following properties.

- •
  $`F_{h}^{k}`$ is fully determined by the samples collected up to the
  end of the $`(h-1)`$-th step of the $`k`$-th episode;
- •
  $`\|F_{h}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{\mathrm{f}}`$ and $`F_{h}^{k}\geq 0`$;
- •
  $`G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ is fully determined by
  the integer $`N(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h)`$ and all samples
  collected up to the end of the $`(h-1)`$-th step of the $`k`$-th
  episode;
- •
  $`0\leq G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\leq C_{\mathrm{g}}`$.

Consider any $`\delta\in(0,1)`$, and introduce the following sequences

|  |  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle X_{k,h}`$ | $`\displaystyle\coloneqq G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)F_{h+1}^{k},\qquad`$ | $`\displaystyle 1\leq k\leq K,1\leq h\leq H+1,`$ |  | (45) |
|  | $`\displaystyle Y_{k,h}`$ | $`\displaystyle\coloneqq c_{h}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)F_{h+1}^{k},\qquad`$ | $`\displaystyle 1\leq k\leq K,1\leq h\leq H+1.`$ |  | (46) |

Then with probability at least $`1-\delta`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}X_{k,h}\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[\big|(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}})F_{h+1}^{k}\big|^{2}\right]\log\frac{T^{HSAB}}{\delta}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\log\frac{T^{HSAB}}{\delta}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{f}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[P_{h}^{k}W_{h+1}^{k}\right]\log\frac{T^{HSAB}}{\delta}}+C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\log\frac{T^{HSAB}}{\delta}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}Y_{k,h}\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{TC_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}}+C_{\mathrm{f}}\log\frac{1}{\delta}`$ |  |

hold simultaneously for all possible collections
$`\{N(s,a,b,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[H]\}`$.

###### Proof B.2

This lemma can also be proved by Freedman’s inequality
(cf. Theorem [4](#Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4 (Freedman’s inequality) ‣ B.1 A user-friendly version of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

- •
  We start by controlling the first term
  $`\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}X_{k,h}`$. It is readily seen that for
  any given
  $`\left\{N(s,a,b,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[H]\right\}`$,
  we have

  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  \mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[X_{i}\right]=\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)F_{h+1}^{k}\right]=0,
  ``` |  |

  where $`\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}`$ denotes the expectation conditioned on
  everything happening before step $`h`$ of the $`k`$-th episode. In
  addition, we make note of the following crude bound:

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\big|X_{k,h}\big|`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\Big|\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)F_{h+1}^{k}\Big|`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\Big(\big\|P^{k}_{h}\big\|_{1}+\big\|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big\|_{1}\Big)\big\|F^{k}_{h+1}\big\|_{\infty}\leq 2C_{\mathrm{f}}C_{\mathrm{g}},`$ |  | (47) |

  which arises from the assumptions
  $`\|F^{k}_{h+1}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{\mathrm{f}}`$,
  $`0\leq G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\leq C_{\mathrm{g}}`$ together with the
  basic fact
  $`\big\|P^{k}_{h}\big\|_{1}=\big\|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big\|_{1}=1`$.
  Additionally, we can calculate that

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[\big|X_{k,h}\big|^{2}\right]`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[\big|(P_{h}^{k}\!\!-\!\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}})F_{h+1}^{k}\big|^{2}\right]`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle{\leq}C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[\big|(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}})F_{h+1}^{k}\big|^{2}\right]`$ |  | (48) |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[\big|P_{h}^{k}F_{h+1}^{k}\big|^{2}\right],`$ |  | (49) |

  where the first inequality comes from the assumption
  $`0\leq G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\leq C_{\mathrm{g}}`$.
  Furthermore,
  ([49](#A2.E49 "In 1st item ‣ Proof B.2 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
  can be expressed as

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[\big|X_{k,h}\big|^{2}\right]`$ | $`\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{a})}{\leq}C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[P_{h}^{k}\big(F_{h+1}^{k}\big)^{2}\right]`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{b})}{\leq}C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big\|F_{h+1}^{k}\big\|_{\infty}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[P_{h}^{k}W_{h+1}^{k}\right]`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{c})}{\leq}C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{f}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[P_{h}^{k}F_{h+1}^{k}\right]`$ |  | (50) |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{f}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\big\|F_{h+1}^{k}\big\|_{\infty}\overset{\mathrm{(d)}}{\leq}HKC_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}=TC_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}.`$ |  | (51) |

  Notably, (a) comes from the fact that $`P_{h}^{k}`$ only has one
  non-zero entry
  (cf. ([15](#A1.E15 "In 2nd item ‣ A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))), (b)
  holds due to the assumption that $`F_{h}^{k}`$ is non-negative,
  whereas (c) and (d) rely on
  $`\|F_{h}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq C_{\mathrm{f}}`$,

  With
  ([47](#A2.E47 "In 1st item ‣ Proof B.2 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  ([50](#A2.E50 "In 1st item ‣ Proof B.2 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  and
  ([51](#A2.E51 "In 1st item ‣ Proof B.2 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  we can invoke
  Theorem [4](#Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4 (Freedman’s inequality) ‣ B.1 A user-friendly version of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
  (with $`m=\lceil\log_{2}T\rceil`$) and take the union bound over all
  possible collections
  $`\big\{N(s,a,b,h)\in[K]\mid(s,a,b,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[H]\big\}`$,
  which have at most $`K^{HSAB}`$ possibilities, to show that: with
  probability at least $`1-\delta`$,

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{k}X_{k,h}\bigg|\lesssim C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\log\frac{K^{HSAB}\log T}{\delta}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sqrt{\max\left\{C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[\big|(P_{h}^{k}\!\!-\!\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}})F_{h+1}^{k}\big|^{2}\right]\!,\!\frac{TC_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}}{2^{m}}\right\}\log\frac{K^{HSAB}\log T}{\delta}}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[\big|(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}})F_{h+1}^{k}\big|^{2}\right]\log\frac{T^{HSAB}}{\delta}}+C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\log\frac{T^{HSAB}}{\delta}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{f}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[P_{h}^{k}F_{h+1}^{k}\right]\log\frac{T^{HSAB}}{\delta}}+C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\log\frac{T^{HSAB}}{\delta}`$ |  |

  holds simultaneously for all
  $`\big\{N(s,a,b,h)\in[K]\mid(s,a,b,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[H]\big\}`$.
- •
  Then we turn to control the second term
  $`\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}Y_{k,h}\right|`$ of interest.
  Similar to $`\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}X_{k,h}\right|`$, we
  have

  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  \displaystyle|Y_{k,h}|\leq 2eC_{\mathrm{f}},
  ``` |  |
  |  |
  ``` math
  \displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[\big|Y_{k,h}\big|^{2}\right]\leq e^{2}TC_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}.
  ``` |  |

  Invoke
  Theorem [4](#Thmtheorem4 "Theorem 4 (Freedman’s inequality) ‣ B.1 A user-friendly version of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
  (with $`m=1`$) to arrive at

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}Y_{k,h}\bigg|\lesssim\sqrt{TC_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}}+C_{\mathrm{f}}\log\frac{1}{\delta}.`$ |  | (52) |

## Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section [A.3.1](#A1.SS3.SSS1 "A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")

### C.1 Proof of Lemma [2](#Thmlemma2 "Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")

The proof is by backward induction. Suppose the bounds hold for the
$`Q`$-values in the $`(h+1)`$-th step, we now establish the bounds for
the $`V`$-values in the $`(h+1)`$-th step and $`Q`$-values in the
$`h`$-th step. For any state $`s`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h+1}^{k}}(\overline{Q}_{h+1}^{k})(s)\overset{(i)}{\geq}\max_{a}\mathbb{E}_{b\sim\nu_{h+1}^{k}}\overline{Q}_{h+1}^{k}(s,a,b)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\geq\max_{a}\mathbb{E}_{b\sim\nu_{h+1}^{k}}Q_{h}^{\dagger,\nu_{h+1}^{k}}(s,a,b)=V_{h+1}^{\dagger,\nu_{h+1}^{k}},`$ |  | (53) |

where (i) comes from the property
([10a](#S3.E10.1 "In 10 ‣ Policy computation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
of CCE. Similarly, we can show
$`\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s)\leq V^{\mu_{h+1}^{k},\dagger}(s)`$.
Therefore, we have: for all $`s`$,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s)\geq V_{h+1}^{\dagger,\nu_{h+1}^{k}}(s)\geq V_{h+1}^{\star}(s)\geq V_{h+1}^{\mu_{h+1}^{k},\dagger}(s)\geq\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s).
``` |  |

Now consider an arbitrary triple $`(s,a,b)`$ in the $`h`$-th step. We
have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Q_{h}^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{k}}(s,a,b)-Q_{h}^{\star}(s,a,b)=\underset{s^{\prime}\sim P_{h}(\cdot\mid s,a,b)}{\mathbb{E}}\left[V_{h+1}^{\dagger,\nu_{h+1}^{k}}(s^{\prime})-V_{h+1}^{\star}\left(s^{\prime}\right)\right]\geq 0.
``` |  | (54) |

Similarly, we can show
$`Q^{\mu_{h}^{k},\dagger}(s,a,b)\leq Q^{\star}(s,a,b)`$. To complete the
induction argument, we should prove

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)\geq Q_{h}^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{k}}(s,a,b),`$ |  | (55) |

which we shall further accomplish by induction. When $`k=1`$,
([55](#A3.E55 "In C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
holds since that the initialization obeys
$`\overline{Q}_{h}^{1}=H\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{1}}(s,a,b)`$ for all
$`(h,a,s,b)\in[H]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}`$.
Next, under the assumption that
([55](#A3.E55 "In C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
holds up to the $`k`$-th episode, we wish to get it for the $`(k+1)`$-th
episode to get Lemma
[2](#Thmlemma2 "Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
According to line 11 and lines 13-14 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
it suffices to justify

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\min{\left\{\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},{k+1}}(s,a,b),Q_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},{k+1}}(s,a,b)\right\}}\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{k+1}}(s,a,b).
``` |  | (56) |

In order to get
([56](#A3.E56 "In C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we need to prove that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle Q_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},{k+1}}(s,a,b)\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{k+1}}(s,a,b),`$ |  | (57) |

and

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},{k+1}}(s,a,b)\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{k+1}}(s,a,b).`$ |  | (58) |

For the sake of convenience, suppose
$`Q_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},k^{\prime}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ and
$`\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},{k^{\prime}}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$
are latest updated in the $`{k}`$-th episode with $`k\leq k^{\prime}`$,
it suffices to verify

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Q^{\mathrm{UCB},{k}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}),
``` |  |

and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},{k}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{k^{\prime}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}).
``` |  |

First, the proof of
([57](#A3.E57 "In C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
is performed.

##### Step 1: decomposing $`Q^{\mathrm{UCB},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$.

Firstly, according to the definition of $`N_{h}^{k}`$ and $`k^{n}`$ in
Appendix [A.1](#A1.SS1 "A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
we can obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Q^{\mathrm{UCB},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=Q^{\mathrm{UCB},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}),
``` |  | (59) |

since $`k^{N_{h}^{k}}=k^{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}=k`$.
According to the update rule (i.e., line 2 in
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "In 𝑄-function estimation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and line 8 in
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle Q^{\mathrm{UCB},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=Q_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\!(1\!\!-\!\eta_{N_{h}^{k}}){Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\eta_{N_{h}^{k}}\left\{r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\!+\!\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}})\!+\!c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}}}\right\}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\!(1\!\!-\!\eta_{N_{h}^{k}}){Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},k^{N_{h}^{k}\!-\!1}\!+\!1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\!+\!\eta_{N_{h}^{k}}\left\{r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\!\!+\!\!\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}})\!\!+\!c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\!\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}}}\right\}\!,`$ |  |

where the last identity again follows from our argument for justifying
([59](#A3.E59 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄^{UCB,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)≥𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Applying this relation recursively and combining with the definitions of
$`\eta_{0}^{N}`$ and $`\eta_{n}^{N}`$ in
([17](#A1.E17 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we obtain

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle{Q}^{\mathrm{UCB},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k}}{Q}^{\mathrm{UCB},1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\bigg\{r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})+c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{n}}\bigg\}.`$ |  | (60) |

With
$`\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k}}+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}=1`$ in
([17](#A1.E17 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([18](#A1.E18 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
there is

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{0}Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}).
``` |  | (61) |

Combining
([60](#A3.E60 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄^{UCB,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)≥𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([61](#A3.E61 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄^{UCB,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)≥𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we can further get

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle{Q}^{\mathrm{UCB},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big({Q}^{\mathrm{UCB},1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\bigg\{r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})+c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{n}}-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\bigg\}.`$ |  | (62) |

To continue, invoking the Bellman optimality equation

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1},
``` |  | (63) |

we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})`$ | $`\displaystyle-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{k^{\prime}}}_{h+1}+P^{k^{n}}_{h}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1}\big),`$ |  | (64) |

where $`P^{k}_{h}`$ is defined in
([15](#A1.E15 "In 2nd item ‣ A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Bringing
([64](#A3.E64 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄^{UCB,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)≥𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
into
([62](#A3.E62 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄^{UCB,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)≥𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
yields

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle{Q}^{\mathrm{UCB},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big({Q}^{\mathrm{UCB},1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\bigg\{\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1}+P^{k^{n}}_{h}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1}\big)+c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{n}}\bigg\}.`$ |  | (65) |

##### Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding $`Q^{\mathrm{UCB},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$.

In order to develop a lower bound on
$`Q^{\mathrm{UCB},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$
based on the decomposition
([65](#A3.Ex39 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄^{UCB,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)≥𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we make note of several simple facts as follows.

- (a)
  The initialization satisfies
  $`{Q}^{\mathrm{UCB},1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq 0`$.
- (b)
  According to
  ([53](#A3.Ex29 "In C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  monotonicity of $`\overline{V}_{h}^{k}`$ in
  ([23](#A1.E23 "In Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
  and $`k\leq k^{\prime}`$, we can obtaion

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  \overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}){\geq}\overline{V}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}_{h+1}{\geq}V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1}.
  ``` |  | (66) |
- (c)
  By the Azuma-Hoeffding inequality and a union bound, we have that with
  probability at least $`1-\delta`$, one has

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  \bigg|\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{k^{\prime}}}_{h+1}\bigg|\leq c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{n}},
  ``` |  | (67) |

Thus,
$`Q^{\mathrm{UCB},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s,a,b)\geq 0`$
and we have concluded the proof of
([57](#A3.E57 "In C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Next is the proof of
([58](#A3.E58 "In C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

##### Step 1: decomposing $`\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$.

Similar to
([59](#A3.E59 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄^{UCB,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)≥𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we can get

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}).
``` |  | (68) |

According to the update rule (i.e., line 6 in
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "In 𝑄-function estimation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and line 9 in
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=(1-\eta_{N_{h}^{k}})\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\eta_{N_{h}^{k}}\bigg\{r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}})-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}})+\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{b}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}\bigg\}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=(1-\eta_{N_{h}^{k}})\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}-1}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\eta_{N_{h}^{k}}\bigg\{r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}})-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}})+\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{b}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}\bigg\},`$ |  |

where the last identity again follows from our argument for justifying
([68](#A3.E68 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Applying this relation recursively and invoking the definitions of
$`\eta_{0}^{N}`$ and $`\eta_{n}^{N}`$ in
([17](#A1.E17 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we are left with

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k}}\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\bigg\{r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})+\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{b}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}\bigg\}.`$ |  | (69) |

Additionally,
([61](#A3.E61 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄^{UCB,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)≥𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
combined with
([69](#A3.E69 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
leads to

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\bigg\{r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\bigg\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\bigg\{\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{b}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\bigg\}.`$ |  | (70) |

To continue, invoking the Bellman optimality equation
([63](#A3.E63 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄^{UCB,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)≥𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and using the construction of $`\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r}}_{h}`$ in
line 12 of
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "In 𝑄-function estimation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
(which is the running mean of $`\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{h+1}`$), we
reach

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle r_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})+\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}})}{n}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1}.`$ |  | (71) |

Next, combined with the definition of

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\varpi^{k^{n}}_{h}\coloneqq\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(P^{k^{i}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}_{h+1},
``` |  | (72) |

we can obtain

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle(\ref{eq:dejavu})`$ | $`\displaystyle=P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\left\{\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\!-\!\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\right\}\!+\!\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\big)}{n}\!-\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1}\!\!+\!\varpi_{h}^{k^{n}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\bigg\{\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1}+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)}{n}\bigg\}+\varpi_{h}^{k^{n}},`$ |  | (73) |

with the notation $`P^{k}_{h}`$ defined in
([15](#A1.E15 "In 2nd item ‣ A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Putting
([73](#A3.E73 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
into
([70](#A3.E70 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
together gives

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\bigg\{P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\bigg(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1}+\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)}{n}\bigg)+\overline{b}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}+\varpi_{h}^{k^{n}}\bigg\}.`$ |  | (74) |

##### Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding $`\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$.

In order to develop a lower bound on
$`\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$
based on the decomposition
([74](#A3.E74 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we make note of several simple facts as follows.

- (a)
  The initialization satisfies
  $`\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq 0`$.
- (b)
  For any $`1\leq k^{n}\leq k^{\prime}`$, one has

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  \overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\geq\overline{V}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}_{h+1}\geq V^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}_{h+1},
  ``` |  | (75) |

  owing to the induction hypotheses of $`Q`$-values in the $`(h+1)`$-th
  step and
  ([53](#A3.Ex29 "In C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
  that holds up to $`k`$.
- (c)
  For all $`0\leq i\leq n`$ and any $`s\in\mathcal{S}`$, one has

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  \overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\geq 0,
  ``` |  | (76) |

  which holds since the reference value
  $`\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{h}(s)`$ is monotonically non-increasing
  in view of the monotonicity of $`\overline{V}_{h}(s)`$ in
  ([23](#A1.E23 "In Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
  and the update rule in line 17 of
  Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").

Based on the three statements above, we can simplify
([74](#A3.E74 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
to

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\big(\overline{b}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}+\varpi^{k^{n}}_{h}\big).
``` |  | (77) |

In the sequel, we aim to establish
$`\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$
based on this inequality
([77](#A3.E77 "In Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
As it turns out, if one could show that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\bigg|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\varpi_{h}^{k^{n}}\bigg|\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\overline{b}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h},
``` |  | (78) |

we claim that
([77](#A3.E77 "In Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
satisfies

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q^{\dagger,\nu_{h}^{{k^{\prime}}+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\overline{b}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}-\bigg|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\varpi^{k^{n}}_{h}\bigg|\geq 0.
``` |  | (79) |

where the first inequality comes from the triangle inequality. As a
result, the remaining steps come down to justifying the assumption
([78](#A3.E78 "In Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
And thus, we need to control the following two quantities (in view of
([72](#A3.E72 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle W_{1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\coloneqq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big),`$ |  | (80a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle W_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\coloneqq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\frac{1}{n}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(P^{k^{i}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}_{h+1}`$ |  | (80b) |

separately, which constitute the next two steps. As will be seen
momentarily, these two terms can be controlled in a similar fashion
using Freedman’s inequality.

##### Step 3: controlling $`W_{1}`$.

In the following, we intend to invoke
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
to control term $`W_{1}`$ defined in
([80a](#A3.E80.1 "In 80 ‣ Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
To begin with, consider any $`(N,h)\in[K]\times[H]`$, and introduce

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle F_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq\overline{V}^{k}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}\qquad\text{and}\qquad G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\coloneqq\eta_{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}^{N}\geq 0.`$ |  | (81) |

Accordingly, we can derive and define the following two equations:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\|F_{h+1}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq\|\overline{V}^{k}_{h+1}\|_{\infty}+\|\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}\|_{\infty}\leq 2H\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{f}},`$ |  | (82) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\max_{N,h,s,a,b\in[K]\times[H]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\eta_{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}^{N}\leq\frac{2H}{N}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{g}},`$ |  | (83) |

where the last inequality comes from

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\eta_{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}^{N}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\frac{2H}{N},\qquad\text{if }1\leq N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)\leq N;`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\eta_{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}^{N}`$ | $`\displaystyle=0,\qquad\quad~\text{if }N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)>N,`$ |  |

along with
Lemma [1](#Thmlemma1 "Lemma 1 ‣ A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and the definition in
([17](#A1.E17 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Moreover, observed from
([18](#A1.E18 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle 0\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}G_{h}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b,N)=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\eta_{n}^{N}\leq 1`$ |  | (84) |

holds for all
$`(N,s,a,b)\in[K]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}`$.
Therefore, applying
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
with the quantities
([81](#A3.E81 "In Step 3: controlling 𝑊_1. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and $`(N,s,a,b)=(N^{k}_{h},s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ implies
that, with probability at least $`1-\delta`$

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle|W_{1}|`$ | $`\displaystyle=\left|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}G_{h}^{k^{n}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},N_{h}^{k})\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(W_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\big)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\left(C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}+\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathrm{g}}}{N}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\right)\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\asymp\sqrt{\frac{H}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)}+\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}},`$ |  | (85) |

where the second equation comes from
$`X_{i}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h,N_{h}^{k})=\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)`$.
Furthermore,
([85](#A3.E85 "In Step 3: controlling 𝑊_1. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
can be simplified to

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle(\ref{lemma1:equ4-sub})\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{H}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\!-\!\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2}}\!+\!\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{(N_{h}^{k})^{3/4}},`$ |  | (86) |

where the proof
([86](#A3.E86 "In Step 3: controlling 𝑊_1. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
is postponed to
Appendix [C.1.1](#A3.SS1.SSS1 "C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
to streamline the presentation.

##### Step 4: controlling $`W_{2}`$.

In the following, our aim is to the quantity $`W_{2}`$ defined in
([80b](#A3.E80.2 "In 80 ‣ Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Rearranging terms in
([80b](#A3.E80.2 "In 80 ‣ Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we obtain

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle W_{2}=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{n}\big(P^{k^{i}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k}}\big)\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}_{h+1}}{n}=\sum_{i=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\left(\sum_{n=i}^{N_{h}^{k}}\frac{\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}}{n}\right)\big(P^{k^{i}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k}}\big)\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}_{h+1},`$ |  |

which can again be controlled by
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
And thus, we abuse the notation by taking

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle F_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}_{h+1}\qquad\text{and}\qquad G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\coloneqq\sum_{n=N_{h}^{i}(s,a,b)}^{N}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N}}{n}\geq 0.`$ |  | (87) |

These quantities satisfy

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\displaystyle\big\|F_{h+1}^{k}\big\|_{\infty}\leq\big\|\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},i}_{h+1}\big\|_{\infty}\leq H\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{f}},
``` |  | (88) |
|  |
``` math
\displaystyle\max_{N,h,s,a,b\in[K]\times[H]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{i}(s,a,b)}^{N}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N}}{n}\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\frac{\eta^{N}_{n}}{n}\leq\frac{2}{N}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{g}}.
``` |  | (89) |

where
([89](#A3.E89 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
holds according to
Lemma [1](#Thmlemma1 "Lemma 1 ‣ A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
Then for all
$`(N,s,a,b)\in[K]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}`$,
it is readily seen from
([89](#A3.E89 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle 0\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}G_{h}^{k_{h}^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b,N)\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N}\frac{2}{N}\leq 2.`$ |  | (90) |

With the above relations in mind, taking
$`(N,s,a,b)=(N_{h}^{k},s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ and applying
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
w.r.t. quantities
([87](#A3.E87 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
reveals that, with probability exceeding $`1-\delta`$

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle|W_{2}|`$ | $`\displaystyle=\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\sum_{n=i}^{N_{h}^{k}}\frac{\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}}{n}\big(P^{k^{i}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},i}_{h+1}\bigg|=\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{k}X_{i}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h,N_{h}^{k})\bigg|`$ |  | (91) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}G_{h}^{k^{n}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},N_{h}^{k})\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(F_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\big)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\left(C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}+\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathrm{g}}}{N}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\right)\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},i}_{h+1}\big)}+\frac{H}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta},`$ |  | (92) |

where the second comes from
$`X_{i}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h,N_{h}^{k})=\sum_{n=i}^{N_{h}^{k}}\frac{\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}}{n}\big(P^{k^{i}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},i}_{h+1}`$
and $`k=N_{h}^{k}`$. Moreover,
([92](#A3.E92 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
can be simplified to

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle(\ref{lemma1:equ5-sub})\!\lesssim\!\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\!\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}\!+\!1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\!\!-\!\!\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}\!+\!1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2}}\!+\!\frac{H}{(N_{h}^{k})^{3/4}}\log^{2}\!\frac{SABT}{\delta},`$ |  | (93) |

where the proof
([93](#A3.E93 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
is postponed to
Appendix [C.1.2](#A3.SS1.SSS2 "C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
to streamline the presentation.

##### Step 5: combining the above bounds.

Combining with the results in
([86](#A3.E86 "In Step 3: controlling 𝑊_1. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([93](#A3.E93 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we obtain an upper bound on
$`\big|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\varpi_{h}^{k^{n}}\big|`$
as follows:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\varpi_{h}^{k^{n}}\bigg|\leq|W_{1}|+|W_{2}|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim~\sqrt{\frac{H}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2}}+\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{(N_{h}^{k})^{3/4}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+c_{\mathrm{b}}\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{(N_{h}^{k})^{3/4}}`$ |  | (94) |

for some sufficiently large constant $`c_{\mathrm{b}}>0`$, where the
last inequality follows from the definition of
$`\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$
in line 15 of
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "In 𝑄-function estimation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").

In order to establish the desired bound
([78](#A3.E78 "In Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we still need to control the sum
$`\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\overline{b}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}`$.
Towards this end, the definition of
$`\overline{b}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}`$
(resp. $`\overline{\delta}_{h}^{\mathrm{R}}`$) in line 8 (resp. line 16)
of
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "In 𝑄-function estimation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
yields

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{b}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}=\Big(1-\frac{1}{\eta_{n}}\Big)\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\frac{1}{\eta_{n}}\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\frac{c_{\mathrm{b}}}{n^{3/4}}H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}.`$ |  | (95) |

This taken collectively with the definition
([17](#A1.E17 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
of $`\eta_{n}^{N}`$ allows us to expand

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\overline{b}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}-c_{\mathrm{b}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}}{n^{3/4}}H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\left(\Big(1-\frac{1}{\eta_{n}}\Big)\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\frac{1}{\eta_{n}}\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\right).`$ |  | (96) |

And thus, we can reach

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle(\ref{eq:eta-b-ref-sum-identity-1})=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\left(-\big(1-\eta_{n}\big)\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\left(\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\prod_{i=n}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\right).`$ |  | (97) |

Under the fact that
$`\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=0`$,
we can reach

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle(\ref{eq:eta-b-ref-sum-identity-2}){=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\sum_{n=2}^{N_{h}^{k}}\prod_{i=n}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle{=}`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}-1}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}),`$ |  | (98) |

where the second equality follows from the fact that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{n=2}^{N_{h}^{k}}\prod_{i=n}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}-1}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n+1}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}-1}\prod_{i=n+1}^{N_{h}^{k}}(1-\eta_{i})\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}).`$ |  |

To be specific, the first relation can be seen by replacing $`n`$ with
$`n+1`$, and the last relation holds true since the state-action pair
$`(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ has not been visited at step $`h`$
between the $`(k^{n}+1)`$-th episode and the $`(k^{n+1}-1)`$-th episode.
Combining the above identity
([98](#A3.E98 "In Step 5: combining the above bounds. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
with the following property (see
Lemma [1](#Thmlemma1 "Lemma 1 ‣ A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\frac{1}{(N_{h}^{k})^{3/4}}\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\frac{\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}}{n^{3/4}}\leq\frac{2}{(N_{h}^{k})^{3/4}},
``` |  |

we can immediately demonstrate that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+c_{\mathrm{b}}\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{(N_{h}^{k})^{3/4}}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}b^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+2c_{\mathrm{b}}\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{(N_{h}^{k})^{3/4}}.`$ |  | (99) |

Taking
([94](#A3.E94 "In Step 5: combining the above bounds. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([99](#A3.E99 "In Step 5: combining the above bounds. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
collectively demonstrates that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\bigg|\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\varpi^{k^{n}}_{h}\bigg|\leq\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+c_{\mathrm{b}}\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{(N_{h}^{k})^{3/4}}\leq\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\overline{b}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}_{h}
``` |  | (100) |

as claimed in
([78](#A3.E78 "In Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
We have thus concluded the proof of
Lemma [2](#Thmlemma2 "Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
based on the argument in Step 2.

#### C.1.1 Proof of inequality ([86](#A3.E86 "In Step 3: controlling 𝑊_1. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))

In order to establish inequality
([86](#A3.E86 "In Step 3: controlling 𝑊_1. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
it suffices to look at the following term

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle W_{3}\!\coloneqq\!\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}\!-\!\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\!-\!\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\!+\!\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2},`$ |  | (101) |

which forms the main content of this subsection.

First of all, according to the update rules of
$`\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{n+1}}_{h}`$ and
$`\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{n+1}}_{h}`$ in lines 13-14 of
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "In 𝑄-function estimation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
we can get

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{n+1}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=(1-\eta_{n})\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{n}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\eta_{n}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big),`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{n+1}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=(1-\eta_{n})\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k^{n}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\eta_{n}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big)^{2}.`$ |  |

Applying this relation recursively and invoking the definitions of
$`\eta_{n}^{N}`$ (resp. $`P_{h}^{k}`$) in
([17](#A1.E17 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
(resp. ([15](#A1.E15 "In 2nd item ‣ A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))),
there is

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{=}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}P_{h}^{k^{n}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big),`$ |  | (102a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{=}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\big)^{2}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}P_{h}^{k^{n}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)^{2}.`$ |  | (102b) |

Recognizing that $`\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}=1`$ (see
([18](#A1.E18 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we can immediately apply Jensen’s inequality to the expressions (i) and
(ii) to yield

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq\Big(\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\Big)^{2}.`$ |  | (103) |

Further, in view of definition
([16](#A1.E16 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we have

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)=P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)^{2}-\left(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)\right)^{2},
``` |  |

which allows one to decompose and bound $`W_{3}`$ as follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle W_{3}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)^{2}-\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}P^{k^{n}}_{h}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\right)^{2}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\Bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}P^{k^{n}}_{h}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\Bigg)^{2}-\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\Big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\Big)^{2}.`$ |  | (104) |

And thus,
([104](#A3.E104 "In C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
reaches to

|     |                                             |     |       |
|-----|---------------------------------------------|-----|-------|
|     | $`\displaystyle W_{3}\leq W_{3,1}+W_{3,2}`$ |     | (105) |

where

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
W_{3,1}=\Bigg|\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)^{2}\Bigg|
``` |  |

and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
W_{3,2}=\bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}P^{k^{n}}_{h}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\bigg)^{2}-\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\Big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\Big)^{2}.
``` |  |

It then boils down to control the above two terms in
([105](#A3.E105 "In C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
separately.

##### Step 1: bounding $`W_{3,1}`$.

To upper bound the term $`W_{3,1}`$ in
([104](#A3.E104 "In C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we resort to
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
by setting

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle F_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq\big(\overline{V}^{i}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},i}_{h+1}\big)^{2}\qquad\text{and}\qquad G_{h}^{i}(s,a,N)\coloneqq\eta_{N_{h}^{i}(s,a,b)}^{N}.`$ |  | (106) |

According to
([83](#A3.E83 "In Step 3: controlling 𝑊_1. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
it is easily seen that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\displaystyle\|F_{h+1}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq\Big(\big\|\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},i}_{h+1}\big\|_{\infty}+\big\|\overline{V}^{i}_{h+1}\big\|_{\infty}\Big)^{2}\leq 4H^{2}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{f}},
``` |  | (107) |
|  |
``` math
\displaystyle\max_{N,h,s,a,b\in[K]\times[H]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\eta_{N_{h}^{i}(s,a,b)}^{N}\leq\frac{2H}{N}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{g}}.
``` |  | (108) |

Armed with the properties
([107](#A3.E107 "In Step 1: bounding 𝑊_{3,1}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([108](#A3.E108 "In Step 1: bounding 𝑊_{3,1}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and recalling
([90](#A3.E90 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we can invoke
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
w.r.t. ([106](#A3.E106 "In Step 1: bounding 𝑊_{3,1}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
$`X_{i}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h,N_{h}^{k})=\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)^{2}`$,
and set $`(N,s,a)=(N_{h}^{k},s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ to yield,
with probability at least $`1-\delta`$

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle W_{3,1}=\Bigg|\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)^{2}\Bigg|`$ |  |  |
````
