````math
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}G_{h}^{k^{n}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},N_{h}^{k})\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(W_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\big)}`$ |  | (109) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\left(C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}+\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathrm{g}}}{N}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\right)\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{H}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\Big(\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)^{2}\Big)}+\frac{H^{3}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}}.`$ |  | (110) |

With $`\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\leq 1`$ (see
([18](#A1.E18 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and the following trivial result:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\Big(\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)^{2}\Big)\leq\big\|\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)^{4}\big\|_{\infty}\leq 16H^{4}.`$ |  | (111) |

We can further obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle W_{3,1}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{H^{5}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+\frac{H^{3}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}.`$ |  | (112) |

##### Step 2: bounding $`W_{3,2}`$.

Jensen’s inequality tells us that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)\Bigg)^{2}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\Bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\big(\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big)^{1/2}\cdot\big(\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big)^{1/2}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)\Bigg)^{2}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\right\}\left\{\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\Big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)\Big)^{2}\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\Big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)\Big)^{2},`$ |  | (113) |

where the last line arises from
([18](#A1.E18 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Substitution into $`W_{3,2}`$
(cf. ([104](#A3.E104 "In C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")))
gives

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle W_{3,2}\leq\bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}P^{k^{n}}_{h}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\bigg)^{2}-\bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\bigg)^{2}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\!\bigg\{\!\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}\!\!-\!\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}\!-\!\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\!\bigg\}\bigg\{\!\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}\!\!+\!\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}\!-\!\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\!\bigg\}.`$ |  | (114) |

In what follows, we would like to use this relation to show that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
W_{3,2}\leq C_{32}\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{H^{5}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+\frac{H^{3}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}\bigg\}
``` |  | (115) |

for some universal constant $`C_{32}>0`$.

If $`W_{3,2}\leq 0`$, then
([115](#A3.E115 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
holds true trivially. Consequently, it is sufficient to study the case
where $`W_{3,2}>0`$. To this end, we first note that the term in the
first pair of curly brakets of
([114](#A3.E114 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
is exactly $`W_{1}`$ (see
([80a](#A3.E80.1 "In 80 ‣ Step 2: two key quantities for lower bounding 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))),
which can be bounded by recalling
([85](#A3.E85 "In Step 3: controlling 𝑊_1. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")):

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle|W_{1}|`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{H}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)}+\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}}`$ |  | (116) |

with probability at least $`1-\delta`$. According to the property that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\Big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\Big)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\big\|\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)^{2}\big\|_{\infty}\leq 4H^{2},`$ |  | (117) |

And thus,
([116](#A3.E116 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
can be simplified as

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle|W_{1}|`$ | $`\displaystyle\!\lesssim\!\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\!\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}}\!+\!\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\!\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}}\!\lesssim\!\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\!\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\!+\!\frac{H^{2}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\!\frac{SABT}{\delta},`$ |  | (118) |

whereas the last inequality
([118](#A3.E118 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
holds as a result of the fact
$`\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{n}\leq 1`$ (see
([18](#A1.E18 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))).

Moreover, the term in the second pair of curly brakets of
([114](#A3.E114 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
can be bounded straightforwardly as follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}+P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)\bigg|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\leq\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\Big(\big\|P^{k^{n}}_{h}\big\|_{1}+\big\|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big\|_{1}\Big)\big\|\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big\|_{\infty}\leq 2H,`$ |  | (119) |

where we have made use of property
([18](#A1.E18 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
as well as the elementary facts
$`\big\|\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big\|_{\infty}\leq H`$
and
$`\big\|P^{k^{n}}_{h}\big\|_{1}=\big\|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big\|_{1}=1`$.
Substituting the above two results
([118](#A3.E118 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([119](#A3.E119 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
back into
([114](#A3.E114 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we arrive at the bound
([115](#A3.E115 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
as long as $`W_{3,2}>0`$. Putting all cases together, we have
established the claim
([115](#A3.E115 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

##### Step 3: putting all this together.

To finish up, plugging the bounds
([112](#A3.E112 "In Step 1: bounding 𝑊_{3,1}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([115](#A3.E115 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{3,2}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
into
([104](#A3.E104 "In C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we can conclude that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle W_{3}\leq W_{3,1}+W_{3,2}\leq C_{3}\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{H^{5}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+\frac{H^{3}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}\bigg\}`$ |  |

for some constant $`C_{3}>0`$. This together with definition
([101](#A3.E101 "In C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
of $`W_{3}`$ results in

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\Big\{\overline{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\big(\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2}\Big\}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+C_{3}\left(\sqrt{\frac{H^{5}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+\frac{H^{3}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}\right),`$ |  |

which combined with the elementary inequality
$`\sqrt{u+v}\leq\sqrt{u}+\sqrt{v}`$ for any $`u,v\geq 0`$ and
([103](#A3.E103 "In C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
yields

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg\{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k}}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)\bigg\}^{1/2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim\Big\{\overline{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\big(\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2}\Big\}^{1/2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{H^{5/4}}{\big(N_{h}^{k}\big)^{1/4}}\log^{1/2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\frac{H^{3/2}}{\big(N_{h}^{k}\big)^{1/2}}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}.`$ |  |

Substitution into
([85](#A3.E85 "In Step 3: controlling 𝑊_1. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
establishes the desired result
([86](#A3.E86 "In Step 3: controlling 𝑊_1. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

#### C.1.2 Proof of inequality ([93](#A3.E93 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))

In order to prove inequality
([93](#A3.E93 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
it suffices to look at the following term

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
W_{4}\coloneqq\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1})-\Big(\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2}\Big).
``` |  | (120) |

In view of the update rules of
$`\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n+1}}_{h}`$ and
$`\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n+1}}_{h}`$ in lines 11-12 of
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "In 𝑄-function estimation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n+1}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\left(1-\frac{1}{n}\right)\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\!\!+\!\!\frac{1}{n}\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1}),`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n+1}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ | $`\displaystyle=\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n}+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\left(1-\frac{1}{n}\right)\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k^{n}}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\!\!+\!\!\frac{1}{n}\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big)^{2}.`$ |  |

Through simple recursion, these identities together with definition
([15](#A1.E15 "In 2nd item ‣ A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
of $`P_{h}^{k}`$ lead to

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{=}\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})=\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}P_{h}^{k^{n}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}},`$ |  | (121a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{=}\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\big)^{2}=\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}P_{h}^{k^{n}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)^{2}.`$ |  | (121b) |

Expressions (i) and (ii) combined with Jensen’s inequality give

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\geq\Big(\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\Big)^{2}.`$ |  | (122) |

Taking these together with the definition

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}})=P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)^{2}-\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)^{2},
``` |  |

we obtain

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle W_{4}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\Big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}})^{2}-\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)^{2}\Big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad-\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}P_{h}^{k^{n}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big)^{2}+\bigg(\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}P_{h}^{k^{n}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\bigg)^{2}.`$ |  | (123) |

And thus,
([124](#A3.E124 "In C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
reaches to

|     |                                          |     |       |
|-----|------------------------------------------|-----|-------|
|     | $`\displaystyle W_{4}=W_{4,1}+W_{4,2}.`$ |     | (124) |

where

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
W_{4,1}=\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}-P^{k^{n}}_{h}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)^{2}
``` |  |

and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
W_{4,2}=\bigg(\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}P_{h}^{k^{n}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\bigg)^{2}-\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\Big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\Big)^{2}
``` |  |

In what follows, we shall bound terms $`W_{4,1}`$ and $`W_{4,2}`$ in
([124](#A3.E124 "In C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
separately.

##### Step 1: bounding $`W_{4,1}`$.

According to
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
the first term $`W_{4,1}`$ in
([124](#A3.E124 "In C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
can be bounded in an almost identical fashion as $`W_{3,1}`$ in
([112](#A3.E112 "In Step 1: bounding 𝑊_{3,1}. ‣ C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Specifically, let us set

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle F_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1})^{2}\qquad\text{and}\qquad G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\coloneqq\frac{1}{N},`$ |  |

which clearly obey

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\|F_{h+1}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq H^{2}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{f}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad|G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)|=\frac{1}{N}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{g}}.`$ |  |

Thus, for all
$`(N,s,a,b)\in[K]\times\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}`$,
there is

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum_{n=1}^{N}G_{h}^{k^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b,N)=\sum_{n=1}^{N}\frac{1}{N}=1
``` |  |

Hence, we can take
$`(N,s,a,b)=(N_{h}^{k},s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ and apply
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
to yield, with probability at least $`1-\delta`$

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle|W_{4,1}|`$ | $`\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)^{2}\bigg|=\left|\sum_{k=1}^{k}X_{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h,N_{h}^{k})\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}G_{h}^{k^{n}}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},N_{h}^{k})\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(W_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\big)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\left(C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}+\sqrt{\frac{C_{\mathrm{g}}}{N}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\right)\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{H^{4}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}}+\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{{N_{h}^{k}}},`$ |  | (125) |

where the first inequality comes from

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
X_{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h,N_{h}^{k})=\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)^{2}
``` |  |

with $`k=\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}`$, and the last inequality results from
the fact that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(W_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\big)\leq\big\|W_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\big\|_{\infty}^{2}\leq C_{\mathrm{f}}^{2}=H^{4}.
``` |  |

##### Step 2: bounding $`W_{4,2}`$.

We now turn to the other term $`W_{4,2}`$ defined in
([124](#A3.E124 "In C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Towards this, we first make the observation that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\Bigg(\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\Bigg)^{2}\leq\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\Big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\Big)^{2},
``` |  | (126) |

which follows from Jensen’s inequality. Based on this relation, we can
upper bound $`W_{4,2}`$ as

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle W_{4,2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\Bigg(\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}P_{h}^{k^{n}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\Bigg)^{2}-\Bigg(\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\Bigg)^{2}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\bigg\{\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P_{h}^{k^{n}}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\bigg\}\bigg\{\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P_{h}^{k^{n}}+P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\bigg\}.`$ |  | (127) |

In the following, we would like to apply this relation to prove

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
W_{4,2}\leq C_{42}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{H^{4}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+\frac{H^{2}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}\bigg)
``` |  | (128) |

for some constant $`C_{42}>0`$.

When $`W_{4,2}\leq 0`$, the claim
([128](#A3.E128 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{4,2}. ‣ C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
holds trivially. As a result, we shall focus on the case where
$`W_{4,2}>0`$. Let us begin with the term in the first pair of curly
brackets of
([127](#A3.E127 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{4,2}. ‣ C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Toward this, let us abuse the notation and set

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle F_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}\qquad\text{and}\qquad G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\coloneqq\frac{1}{N},`$ |  |

which satisfy

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\|F_{h+1}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq H\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{f}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad|G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)|=\frac{1}{N}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{g}}.
``` |  |

Akin to our argument for bounding $`W_{4,1}`$, invoking
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and setting $`(N,s,a,b)=(N_{h}^{k},s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$
imply that, with probability at least $`1-\delta`$, there is

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\Bigg|\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}(P_{h}^{k^{n}}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}})\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\Bigg|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}}+\frac{H\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}.
``` |  |

In addition, under the fact that
$`\big\|\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big\|_{\infty}\leq H`$ and
$`\big\|P^{k^{n}}_{h}\big\|_{1}=\big\|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big\|_{1}=1`$,
the term in the second pair of curly brackets of
([127](#A3.E127 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{4,2}. ‣ C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
can be straightly bounded by

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\Bigg|\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\big(P_{h}^{k^{n}}+P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\Bigg|\leq\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\big(\big\|P^{k^{n}}_{h}\big\|_{1}+\big\|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big\|_{1}\big)\big\|\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\big\|_{\infty}\leq 2H.
``` |  |

We have thus finished the proof of the claim
([128](#A3.E128 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{4,2}. ‣ C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

##### Step 3: putting all pieces together.

Combining the results
([125](#A3.Ex8b "In Step 1: bounding 𝑊_{4,1}. ‣ C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([128](#A3.E128 "In Step 2: bounding 𝑊_{4,2}. ‣ C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
with
([124](#A3.E124 "In C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
yields

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle W_{4}\leq|W_{4,1}|+W_{4,2}\leq C_{4}\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{H^{4}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+\frac{H^{2}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}\bigg\}`$ |  |

for some constant $`C_{4}>0`$. Taking the definition
([120](#A3.E120 "In C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
of $`W_{4}`$ together, this bound gives

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}})`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\Big\{\overline{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\big(\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2}\Big\}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+C_{4}\bigg\{\sqrt{\frac{H^{4}}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+\frac{H^{2}}{N_{h}^{k}}\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}\bigg\}.`$ |  |

Invoke the elementary inequality $`\sqrt{u+v}\leq\sqrt{u}+\sqrt{v}`$ for
any $`u,v\geq 0`$ and use the property
([122](#A3.E122 "In C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
to obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}({V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}})\bigg)^{1/2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\Big\{\overline{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\big(\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)^{2}\Big\}^{1/2}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{H}{(N_{h}^{k})^{1/4}}\log^{1/2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\frac{H}{(N_{h}^{k})^{1/2}}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}.`$ |  |

Substitution into
([92](#A3.E92 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
directly establishes the desired result
([93](#A3.E93 "In Step 4: controlling 𝑊_2. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

### C.2 Proof of Lemma [3](#Thmlemma3 "Lemma 3 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")

Before the proof of
([25](#A1.E25 "In Lemma 3 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we present the following
Lemma [9](#Thmlemma9 "Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
for an auxiliary illustration. It is worth noting that, similar to
([Yang et al., 2021](#bib.bib10), Lemma 4.2) (see also ([Jin et al.,
2018b](#bib.bib11), Lemma C.7)),
Lemma [9](#Thmlemma9 "Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
is essentially an algebraic result leveraging certain relations,
w.r.t. the $`Q`$-value estimates.

###### Lemma 9

Assume there exists a constant $`c>0`$ such that for all
$`(s,a,b,k,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[K]\times[H]`$,
it holds that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle 0\leq\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s)-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s)\leq\overline{Q}^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)-\underline{Q}^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)+\zeta_{h}^{k}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}_{0}H+\!\!\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}_{n}\left(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\underline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\right)+4c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}}+\zeta_{h}^{k}.`$ |  | (129) |

Consider any $`\varepsilon\in(0,H]`$. Then for all
$`\beta=1,\ldots,\left\lceil\log_{2}\frac{H}{\varepsilon}\right\rceil`$,
one has

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\Bigg|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbbm{1}\left(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k})\in\big[2^{\beta-1}\varepsilon,2^{\beta}\varepsilon\big)\right)\Bigg|\lesssim\frac{H^{6}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{4^{\beta}\varepsilon^{2}}.
``` |  | (130) |

The proof of lemma
[9](#Thmlemma9 "Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
will be carried in Appendix
[C.2.1](#A3.SS2.SSS1 "C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
We first show how to justify
([25](#A1.E25 "In Lemma 3 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
if inequality
([130](#A3.E130 "In Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
holds. As can be seen, the fact
([130](#A3.E130 "In Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
immediately leads to

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbbm{1}\left(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})>\varepsilon\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{\beta=1}^{\left\lceil\log_{2}\frac{H}{\varepsilon}\right\rceil}\frac{H^{6}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{4^{\beta}\varepsilon^{2}}\leq\frac{H^{6}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{2\varepsilon^{2}}`$ |  | (131) |

as desired.

#### C.2.1 Proof of Lemma [9](#Thmlemma9 "Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")

We first return to justify the claim
([129](#A3.Ex23a "In Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Lemma [2](#Thmlemma2 "Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and
Lemma [3](#Thmlemma3 "Lemma 3 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
directly verify the left-hand side of
([129](#A3.Ex23a "In Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
since

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{Q}_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)\geq Q_{h}^{\star}(s,a,b)\geq\underline{Q}_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)\qquad\text{for all }(s,a,b,k,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}\times[K]\times[H].
``` |  | (132) |

The remainder of the proof is thus devoted to justifying the upper bound
on $`\overline{Q}^{k+1}_{h}(s,a,b)-\underline{Q}^{k+1}_{h}(s,a,b)`$ in
([129](#A3.Ex23a "In Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Then we reach

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}_{h}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s,a,b)-\underline{Q}_{h}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1}(s,a,b)\leq{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s,a,b)-{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{LCB},k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s,a,b)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad=(1-\eta_{N_{h}^{k}})\Big({Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s,a,b)-{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{LCB},k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s,a,b)\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\quad+\eta_{N_{h}^{k}}\Bigg(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}+1})+\left(P_{h,s,a,b}-P_{h}^{k^{n}}\right)\left(\overline{V}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}_{h+1}-\underline{V}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}_{h+1}\right)\Bigg),`$ |  |

where we abbreviate

|     |                            |     |
|-----|----------------------------|-----|
|     |
       ``` math
       N_{h}^{k}=N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)
       ```                         |     |

throughout this subsection as long as it is clear from the context.

Applying this relation recursively leads to the desired result

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}_{h}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s,a,b)-\underline{Q}_{h}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}(s,a,b)=\eta^{N^{k}_{h}}_{0}\Big({Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},1}(s,a,b)-{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{LCB},1}(s,a,b)\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}}_{n}\Bigg(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}+1})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})+\left(P_{h,s,a,b}-P_{h}^{k^{n}}\right)\left(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}-\underline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}\right)\Bigg)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\leq\eta^{N^{k}_{h}}_{0}H+\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}}_{n}\left(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\underline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\right)+4c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}}}.`$ |  |

Here, the last line is valid due to the property
$`\underline{Q}_{h}^{1}(s,a,b)=0`$ and
$`\overline{Q}_{h}^{1}(s,a,b)=H`$,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\Big\lvert\left(P_{h,s,a,b}-P_{h}^{k^{n}}\right)\overline{V}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}_{h+1}\Big\rvert\leq c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}}}
``` |  |

and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\Big\lvert\left(P_{h,s,a,b}-P_{h}^{k^{n}}\right)\underline{V}^{k^{N_{h}^{k}}}_{h+1}\Big\rvert\leq c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}}}
``` |  |

based on the Azuma-Hoeffding inequality and a union bound, and the
following fact

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}}_{n}c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}}}\leq 2c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}}},
``` |  |

which is an immediate consequence of the elementary property
$`\sum_{n=1}^{N}\frac{\eta_{n}^{N}}{\sqrt{n}}\leq\frac{2}{\sqrt{N}}`$
(see
Lemma [1](#Thmlemma1 "Lemma 1 ‣ A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Combined with

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})\leq\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\underline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\zeta_{h}^{k},
``` |  |

this establishes the condition
([129](#A3.Ex23a "In Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

Afterwards we prove
([130](#A3.E130 "In Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Accounting for the difference between our algorithm and the one in [Yang
et al. (2021)](#bib.bib10), we paraphrase ([Yang et al.,
2021](#bib.bib10), Definition 4.2) into the following form that is
convenient for our purpose.

###### Definition 3 ($`(C,w)`$-Sequence)

A sequence $`\{w_{k}\}_{1\leq k\leq K}`$ is called a $`(C,w)`$-Sequence
if $`0\leq w_{k}\leq w`$ for all $`k`$ and $`\sum_{k}w_{k}\leq C`$.

Combining with
([129](#A3.Ex23a "In Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\big(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)\big)\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\big(\overline{Q}^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)-\underline{Q}^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)\big)+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\zeta_{h}^{k}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\bigg(\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}_{0}H+\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}_{n}\left(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\underline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\right)\bigg)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\zeta_{h}^{k}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}4w_{k}c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}_{0}H+\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}_{n}\left(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\underline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\zeta_{h}^{k}+4\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}}.`$ |  | (133) |

For the first term of
([133](#A3.E133 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
$`N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)`$ is at most once for every state-action pair, and we
always have $`w_{k}\leq w`$. Thus

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}_{0}H=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}H\mathbbm{1}\{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)=0\}\leq wSABH.
``` |  | (134) |

For the second term in
([133](#A3.E133 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we exchange the order of summation and obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}_{n}\left(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\underline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad=\sum_{l=1}^{K}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{l}(s_{h+1}^{l})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{l}(s_{h+1}^{l})\Big)\sum_{j=N_{h}^{l}+1}^{N_{h}^{K}(s_{h}^{l},a_{h}^{l},b_{h}^{l})}w_{k^{j}}\eta^{j}_{N_{h}^{l}+1}.`$ |  |

Then for $`l\in[K]`$, we let
$`\widetilde{w}_{l}=\sum_{j=N_{h}^{l}+1}^{N_{h}^{K}(s_{h}^{l},a_{h}^{l},b_{h}^{l})}w_{k^{j}}\eta^{j}_{N_{h}^{l}+1}`$
and further simplify the above equation to be

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)}_{n}\left(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\underline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1}),\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{K}\widetilde{w}_{l}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{l}(s,a,b)-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{l}(s,a,b)\Big).`$ |  | (135) |

Next, we use Lemma
[1](#Thmlemma1 "Lemma 1 ‣ A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
to verify that $`\{\widetilde{w}\}_{l\in[K]}`$ is a
$`(C,(1+\frac{1}{H})w)`$-sequence:

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\widetilde{w}_{l}\leq w\sum_{j=N_{h}^{l}+1}^{N_{h}^{K}\left(s_{h}^{l},a_{h}^{l},b_{h}^{l}\right)}\eta^{j}_{N_{h}^{l}+1}\leq w\sum_{j\geq N_{h}^{l}+1}\eta^{j}_{N_{h}^{l}+1}\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)w,
``` |  |

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{K}\widetilde{w}_{l}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{K}\sum_{j=N_{h}^{l}+1}^{N_{h}^{K}\left(s_{h}^{l},a_{h}^{l},b_{h}^{l}\right)}w_{k^{j}}\eta^{j}_{N_{h}^{l}+1}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\sum_{t=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\eta^{N_{h}^{k}}_{t}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\leq C.`$ |  | (136) |

For the third term of
([133](#A3.E133 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we know that $`\zeta_{h}^{k}`$ is a martingale difference sequence
(w.r.t both $`h`$ and $`k`$), and
$`\left\lvert\zeta_{h}^{k}\right\rvert\leq 4H`$. Hence, by the Hoeffding
inequality, we have with probability at least $`1-\delta/2`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}{w}_{k}\zeta_{h}^{k}\leq\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}{w}_{k}H^{2}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\leq\sqrt{CH^{2}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}.`$ |  | (137) |

The last term of
([133](#A3.E133 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
can be bounded by the following inequalities with respective reasons
listed below:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle 4\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=4\sum_{(s,a,b)}\sum^{K}_{\begin{subarray}{c}k=1\\
(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=(s,a,b)\end{subarray}}w_{k}c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}(s,a,b)}}`$ |  | (138) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle{=}4c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{(s,a,b)}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{K}(s,a,b)}\frac{w_{k^{n}}}{\sqrt{n}}`$ |  | (139) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{(i)}{\leq}4c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{(s,a,b)}\sum_{n=1}^{\left\lceil C_{s,a,b}/w\right\rceil}\frac{w}{\sqrt{n}}`$ |  | (140) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{(ii)}{\leq}10c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{(s,a,b)}\sqrt{C_{s,a,b}w}`$ |  | (141) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{(iii)}{\leq}10c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{H^{3}SABCw\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}},`$ |  | (142) |

where (i) follows from a rearrangement inequality with $`C_{s,a,b}`$
defined as $`C_{s,a,b}\coloneqq\sum_{i=1}^{N_{h}^{K}(s,a,b)}w_{k^{n}}`$
and we always keep in mind that $`0<w_{k^{n}}\leq w`$, (ii) follows from
the integral conversion of $`\sum_{i}1/\sqrt{i}`$, and (iii) is true
because of Cauchy-Schwartz inequality with
$`\sum_{(s,a,b)}C_{s,a,b}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\leq C`$.

Plugging the upper bounds of three separate terms in
([134](#A3.E134 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
([135](#A3.E135 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
([137](#A3.E137 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([142](#A3.E142 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
back into
([133](#A3.E133 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
gives us

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\big(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)-`$ | $`\displaystyle\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)\big)\leq wSABH+10c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{H^{3}SABCw\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sqrt{CH^{2}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+\sum_{l=1}^{K}\widetilde{w}_{l}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{l}(s,a,b)-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{l}(s,a,b)\Big),`$ |  | (143) |

where the third term is a weighted sum of learning errors of the same
format, but taken at level $`h+1`$. In addition, it has weights
$`\{\widetilde{w}\}_{l\in[K]}`$ being a
$`(C,(1+\frac{1}{H})w)`$-sequence. Under recursing, the above analysis
will also yield

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}\big(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s,a,b)\big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h^{\prime}}^{H-h}\Big(SABH(1+\frac{1}{H})^{h^{\prime}}w+\sqrt{CH^{2}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+10c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{H^{3}SABC(1+\frac{1}{H})^{h^{\prime}}w\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\Big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle H\Big(SABHew+\sqrt{CH^{2}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+10c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{H^{3}SABCew\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\Big).`$ |  | (144) |

For every $`n\in[N]`$, $`h\in[H]`$, let

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle w_{k}^{(n,h)}`$ | $`\displaystyle\coloneqq\mathbbm{1}\left[\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h}^{k}\right)\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)\in\left[2^{n-1}\varepsilon,2^{n}\varepsilon\right)\right],`$ |  | (145) |
|  | $`\displaystyle C^{(n,h)}`$ | $`\displaystyle\coloneqq\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}^{(n,h)}=\left|\left\{k:\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h}^{k}\right)\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)\in\left[2^{n-1}\varepsilon,2^{n}\varepsilon\right)\right\}\right|.`$ |  | (146) |

By definition, $`\forall h\in[H]`$ and $`n\in[N]`$,
$`\{w_{k}^{(n,h)}\}_{k\in[K]}`$ is a $`(C^{(n,h)},1)`$-sequence. Now we
consider bounding
$`\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}^{(n,h)}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h}^{k}\right)\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)`$
from both sides. On the one hand, by
([144](#A3.E144 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}^{(n,h)}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h}^{k}\right)\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)\leq eH^{2}SAB`$ | $`\displaystyle+\sqrt{H^{4}C^{(n,h)}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+10c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{eH^{5}SABC^{(n,h)}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}.`$ |  |

On the other hand, according to the definition of $`w_{k}^{(n,h)}`$,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum_{k=1}^{K}w_{k}^{(n,h)}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h}^{k}\right)\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)\geq(2^{n-1}\varepsilon)C^{(n,h)}.
``` |  |

Combining these two sides, we obtaion the following inequality of
$`C^{(n,h)}`$:

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
(2^{n-1}\varepsilon)C^{(n,h)}\leq eH^{2}SAB+\sqrt{H^{4}C^{(n,h)}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+10c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{eH^{5}SABC^{(n,h)}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}},
``` |  |

and thus,

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
C^{(n,h)}\lesssim\frac{H^{5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{4^{n}\varepsilon^{2}}.
``` |  |

Finally, we observe that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Bigg|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbbm{1}\left(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\in\big[2^{\beta-1}\varepsilon,2^{\beta}\varepsilon\big)\right)\Bigg|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle C^{(\beta)}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}C^{(\beta,h)}\lesssim\frac{H^{6}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{4^{\beta}\varepsilon^{2}}.`$ |  | (147) |

Thus we obtain
([130](#A3.E130 "In Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and conclude the proof of the inequality in Lemma
[9](#Thmlemma9 "Lemma 9 ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").

### C.3 Proof of Lemma [4](#Thmlemma4 "Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")

#### C.3.1 Proof of the inequality ([27](#A1.E27 "In Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))

Consider any state $`s`$ that has been visited at least once during the
$`K`$ episodes. Throughout this proof, we shall adopt the shorthand
notation

|     |                     |     |
|-----|---------------------|-----|
|     |
       ``` math
       k^{i}=k_{h}^{i}(s),
       ```                  |     |

which denotes the index of the episode in which state $`s`$ is visited
for the $`i`$-th time at step $`h`$. Given that $`\overline{V}_{h}(s)`$
and $`\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)`$ are only updated during the
episodes with indices coming from $`\{i\mid 1\leq k^{i}\leq K\}`$, it
suffices to show that for any $`s`$ and the corresponding
$`1\leq k^{i}\leq K`$, the claim
([27](#A1.E27 "In Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
holds in the sense that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big|\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s)-\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}+1}(s)\big|\leq 2.`$ |  | (148) |

Towards this end, we look at three scenarios separately.

##### Case 1.

Suppose that $`k^{i}`$ obeys

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s)-\underline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s)>1`$ |  | (149) |

or

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s)-\underline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s)\leq 1\qquad\text{and}\qquad{u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k^{i}}(s)=\mathsf{True}.`$ |  | (150) |

The above conditions correspond to the ones in line 17 and line 19 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
which means that $`\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R}}_{h}`$ is updated during the
$`k^{i}`$-th episode. Thus, it results in

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s)=\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}+1}(s).
``` |  |

This satisfies
([148](#A3.E148 "In C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
obviously.

##### Case 2.

Suppose that $`k^{i_{0}}`$ is the first time such that
([149](#A3.E149 "In Case 1. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([150](#A3.E150 "In Case 1. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
are violated, namely,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle{i_{0}}\coloneqq\min\left\{j\mid\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{j}+1}(s)-\underline{V}_{h}^{k^{j}+1}(s)\leq 1~\text{ and }~{u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k^{j}}(s)=\mathsf{False}\right\}.`$ |  | (151) |

We make several observations. Firstly, combined with the update rules
(lines 16-19 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
the definition
([151](#A3.E151 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
reveals that $`\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R}}`$ has been updated in the
$`k^{i_{0}-1}`$-th episode, thus indicating that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i_{0}}}(s)=\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i_{0}-1}+1}(s)=\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i_{0}-1}+1}(s)=\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i_{0}}}(s).`$ |  | (152) |

Additionally, $`\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)`$ is not updated during
the $`k^{i_{0}}`$-th episode according to the definition
([151](#A3.E151 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
namely,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i_{0}}+1}(s)=\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i_{0}}}(s).`$ |  | (153) |

Therefore, the definition of $`k^{i_{0}}`$ indicates that either
([149](#A3.E149 "In Case 1. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
or
([150](#A3.E150 "In Case 1. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
is satisfied in the previous episode $`k^{i}=k^{i_{0}-1}`$ in which
$`s`$ was visited. If
([149](#A3.E149 "In Case 1. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
is satisfied, then lines 18-19 in
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
tell us that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathsf{True}={u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k^{i_{0}-1}+1}(s)={u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k^{i_{0}}}(s),`$ |  | (154) |

which, however, contradicts the assumption
$`{u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k^{i_{0}}}(s)=\mathsf{False}`$ in
([151](#A3.E151 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Therefore, in the $`k^{i_{0}-1}`$-th episode,
([150](#A3.E150 "In Case 1. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
is satisfied, thus leading to

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i_{0}}}(s)-\underline{V}_{h}^{k^{i_{0}}}(s)=\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i_{0}-1}+1}(s)-\underline{V}_{h}^{k^{i_{0}-1}+1}(s)\leq 1.`$ |  | (155) |

From
([152](#A3.E152 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
([153](#A3.E153 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
and
([155](#A3.E155 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we see that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i_{0}}+1}_{h}(s)-\overline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}+1}_{h}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle=\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i_{0}}}_{h}(s)-\overline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}+1}_{h}(s)=\overline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}}_{h}(s)-\overline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}+1}_{h}(s)`$ |  | (156) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\leq}\overline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}}_{h}(s)-\underline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}}_{h}(s)\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\leq}1,`$ |  | (157) |

where (i) holds since
$`\overline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}+1}_{h}(s)\geq V^{\star}_{h}(s)\geq\overline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}}_{h}(s)`$,
and (ii) follows from
([155](#A3.E155 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
In addition, we make note of the fact that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i_{0}}+1}_{h}(s)-\overline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}+1}_{h}(s)=\overline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}}_{h}(s)-\overline{V}^{k^{i_{0}}+1}_{h}(s)\geq 0,`$ |  | (158) |

which follows from
([156](#A3.E156 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and the monotonicity of $`\overline{V}_{h}^{k}(s)`$ in $`k`$. With the
above results in place, we arrive at the advertised bound
([148](#A3.E148 "In C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
when $`i=i_{0}`$.

##### Case 3.

Consider any $`i>i_{0}`$. It is easy to verify that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s)-\underline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s)\leq 1\qquad\text{and}\qquad{u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k^{i}}(s)=\mathsf{False}.`$ |  | (159) |

It then follows that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}+1}(s)`$ | $`\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i_{0}}+1}(s)\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\leq}\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i_{0}}+1}(s)+1\overset{(\mathrm{iii})}{\leq}\underline{V}_{h}^{k^{i_{0}}+1}(s)+2.`$ |  | (160) |

Here, (i) holds due to the monotonicity of
$`\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R}}`$ and $`\overline{V}_{h}^{k}`$ (see
line 15 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
(ii) is a consequence of
([157](#A3.E157 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
(iii) comes from the definition
([151](#A3.E151 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
of $`i_{0}`$. Further, according to see
Lemma [2](#Thmlemma2 "Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and
Lemma [3](#Thmlemma3 "Lemma 3 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
([160](#A3.E160 "In Case 3. ‣ C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
can be expressed as

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}+1}(s){\leq}V_{h}^{\star}(s)+2{\leq}\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s)+2,`$ |  | (161) |

where the first inequality arises since $`\underline{V}_{h}`$ is a lower
bound on $`V^{\star}_{h}`$ (see
Lemma [3](#Thmlemma3 "Lemma 3 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
and the last inequality is valid since
$`\overline{V}^{k^{i}+1}_{h}(s)\geq V^{\star}_{h}(s)`$ (see
Lemma [2](#Thmlemma2 "Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
In addition, in view of the monotonicity of $`\overline{V}_{h}^{k}`$
(see line 15 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and the update rule in line 17 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
we know that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}+1}(s)\geq\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s).
``` |  |

The preceding two bounds taken collectively demonstrate that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
0\leq\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}+1}(s)-\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{i}+1}(s)\leq 2,
``` |  |

thus justifying
([148](#A3.E148 "In C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
for this case.

Therefore, we have established
([148](#A3.E148 "In C.3.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
for all cases, and hence
([27](#A1.E27 "In Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
also holds for all cases.

#### C.3.2 Proof of the inequality ([28](#A1.E28 "In Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))

Suppose that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h}(s^{k}_{h})\neq 0`$ |  | (162) |

holds for some $`k<K`$. Then there are two possible scenarios to look
at:

##### Case 1.

If the condition in line 16 and line 18 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
are violated at step $`h`$ of the $`k`$-th episode, this means that we
have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})\leq 1\quad\text{ and }\quad u_{\mathrm{r}}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})=\mathsf{False}
``` |  | (163) |

in this case. Then for any $`k^{\prime}>k`$, one necessarily has

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\begin{cases}\overline{V}_{h}^{k^{\prime}}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k^{\prime}}(s_{h}^{k})\leq\overline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})\leq 1,\\
{u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k^{\prime}}(s_{h}^{k})={u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})=\mathsf{False},\end{cases}`$ |  | (164) |

where the first property makes use of the monotonicity of
$`\overline{V}_{h}^{k}`$ and $`\underline{V}_{h}^{k}`$ (see
([23](#A1.E23 "In Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))).
In turn, Condition
([164](#A3.E164 "In Case 1. ‣ C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
implies that $`\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R}}`$ will no longer be updated
after the $`k`$-th episode (see line 16 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
thus indicating that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h})=\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},{k+1}}_{h}(s^{k}_{h})=\cdots=\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h}(s^{k}_{h}).`$ |  | (165) |

This, however, contradicts the assumption
([162](#A3.E162 "In C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").

##### Case 2.

If the condition in either line 16 or line 18 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
is satisfied at step $`h`$ of the $`k`$-th episode, then the update rule
in line 16 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
implies that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})>1,
``` |  | (166) |

or

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})\leq 1\qquad\text{and}\quad{u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})=\mathsf{True}.`$ |  | (167) |

To summarize, the above argument demonstrates that
([162](#A3.E162 "In C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
can only occur if either
([166](#A3.E166 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
or
([167](#A3.E167 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
holds. With the above observation in place, we can proceed with the
following decomposition:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k})-\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},K}(s_{h}^{k})\right)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k})-\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},K}(s_{h}^{k})\right)\mathbbm{1}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k})-\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},K}(s_{h}^{k})\neq 0\right)=\omega_{1}+\omega_{2},`$ |  | (168) |

where

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\omega_{1}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k})-\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},K}(s_{h}^{k})\right)\mathbbm{1}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})\leq 1\text{ and }{u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})=\mathsf{True}\right)
``` |  |

and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\omega_{2}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},K}(s_{h}^{k})\right)\mathbbm{1}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})>1\right).
``` |  |

Regarding $`\omega_{1}`$ in
([168](#A3.E168 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
it is readily seen that for all $`s\in\mathcal{S}`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbbm{1}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s)-\underline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s)\leq 1\text{ and }{u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k}(s)=\mathsf{True}\right)\leq 1,`$ |  | (169) |

which arises since, for each $`s\in\mathcal{S}`$, the above condition is
satisfied in at most one episode, owing to the monotonicity property of
$`\overline{V}_{h},\underline{V}_{h}`$ and the update rule for
$`{u}_{\mathrm{r}}`$ in line 18 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
As a result, one has

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\omega_{1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbbm{1}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s_{h}^{k})\leq 1\text{ and }{u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})=\mathsf{True}\right)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbbm{1}\left(\overline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s)-\underline{V}_{h}^{k+1}(s)\leq 1\text{ and }{u}_{\mathrm{r}}^{k}(s)=\mathsf{True}\right)\leq H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{s\in\mathcal{S}}1=H^{2}S,`$ |  |

where the first inequality holds since
$`\|\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}-\underline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},K}\|_{\infty}\leq H`$.
Substitution into
([168](#A3.E168 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
yields

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(V_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k})-V_{h}^{\mathrm{R},K}(s_{h}^{k})\right)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq H^{2}S+\omega_{2}.`$ |  | (170) |

To complete the proof, it boils down to bounding the term $`\omega_{2}`$
defined in
([168](#A3.E168 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
To begin with, note that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h}(s^{k}_{h})\geq V^{\star}_{h}(s^{k}_{h})\geq\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k}),
``` |  |

where we make use of the optimism of
$`\overline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},K}(s^{k}_{h})`$ stated in
Lemma [2](#Thmlemma2 "Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
(cf. ([20](#A1.E20 "In Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")))
and the pessimism of $`\underline{V}_{h}`$ in
Lemma [2](#Thmlemma2 "Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
(see
([21](#A1.E21 "In Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))).
As a result, we can obtain

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\omega_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k})\right)\mathbbm{1}\left(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k})>1\right).`$ |  | (171) |

Further, let us make note of the following elementary identity

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbbm{1}\Big(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})>t\Big)\mathrm{d}t.
``` |  |

This allows us to obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\omega_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bigg\{\left\{\int_{0}^{\infty}\mathbbm{1}\big(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})>t\big)\mathrm{d}t\right\}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\times\mathbbm{1}\Big(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})>1\Big)\bigg\}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\int_{1}^{H}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbbm{1}\Big(\overline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})>t\Big)\mathrm{d}t.`$ |  |

With the property
([25](#A1.E25 "In Lemma 3 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
in
Lemma [3](#Thmlemma3 "Lemma 3 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
we can further obtain

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\omega_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{1}^{H}\frac{H^{6}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{t^{2}}\mathrm{d}t\lesssim H^{6}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}.`$ |  | (172) |

Combining the above bounds
([171](#A3.E171 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([172](#A3.E172 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
with
([170](#A3.E170 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
establishes

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h}(s^{k}_{h})\right)\lesssim H^{2}S+H^{6}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}\lesssim H^{6}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |

as claimed.

## Appendix D Proof of Lemma [5](#Thmlemma5 "Lemma 5 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
````
