````math
A starting point for proving this lemma is the upper bound already
derived in
([31](#A1.E31 "In A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
and we intend to further bound the first term on the right-hand side of
([31](#A1.E31 "In A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Recalling the expression of
$`\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ in
([70](#A3.E70 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([71](#A3.E71 "In Step 1: decomposing 𝑄̄^{R,𝑘+1}_ℎ⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘)-𝑄^{†,𝜈_ℎ^{𝑘^′+1}}⁢(𝑠_ℎ^𝑘,𝑎_ℎ^𝑘,𝑏_ℎ^𝑘). ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we can derive

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-{Q}_{h}^{\star}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q_{h}^{\star}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  | (173) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\left(\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q_{h}^{\star}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\right)+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\overline{b}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\bigg(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\bigg)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}})-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\star}\bigg).`$ |  |  |

Specifically, according to
([99](#A3.E99 "In Step 5: combining the above bounds. ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
with
$`\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k-1}}+1}_{h}=\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h}`$
and the initialization
$`\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=H`$,
there is

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-{Q}_{h}^{\star}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  | (174) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}H+\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\frac{2c_{\mathrm{b}}H^{2}}{{\big(N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)}^{3/4}}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\bigg(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\bigg)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}})-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\star}\bigg).`$ |  |  |

We can also demonstrate

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\underline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-{Q}_{h}^{\star}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=\underline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}+1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q_{h}^{\star}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  | (175) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\left(\underline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-Q_{h}^{\star}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\right)-\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\underline{b}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}+1}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\bigg(\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\bigg)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}})-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\star}\bigg).`$ |  |  |

Similarly with
$`\underline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k^{N_{h}^{k-1}}+1}_{h}=\underline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h}`$
and the initialization
$`\underline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},1}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})=H`$,
there is

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\underline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-{Q}_{h}^{\star}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$ |  | (176) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\geq`$ | $`\displaystyle-\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}H-\underline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\frac{2c_{\mathrm{b}}H^{2}}{{\big(N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big)}^{3/4}}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\bigg(\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\bigg)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}})-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\star}\bigg).`$ |  |  |

Summing over all $`1\leq k\leq K`$, taking
([174](#A4.E174 "In Appendix D Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([176](#A4.E176 "In Appendix D Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
collectively demonstrates that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}(\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\underline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}))`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(2H\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}+\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)+\underline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\frac{4c_{\mathrm{b}}H^{2}}{\left(N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)\right)^{3/4}}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)\right).`$ |  | (177) |

Next, we control each term in
([177](#A4.E177 "In Appendix D Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
separately.

- •
  Regarding the first term of
  ([177](#A4.E177 "In Appendix D Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  we make two observations. To begin with,

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\eta^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}_{0}\leq\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\sum_{n=0}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s,a,b)}\eta^{n}_{0}\leq SAB,`$ |  | (178) |

  where the last inequality follows since $`\eta_{0}^{n}=0`$ for all
  $`n>0`$ (see
  ([17](#A1.E17 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))).
  Next, it is also observed that

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{1}{\big(N^{k-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{3/4}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s,a,b)}\frac{1}{n^{3/4}}`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}4\big(N_{h}^{K-1}(s,a,b)\big)^{1/4}\leq 4(SAB)^{3/4}K^{1/4},`$ |  | (179) |

  where the last inequality comes from Holder’s inequality

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\big(N_{h}^{K-1}(s,a,b)\big)^{1/4}`$ |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\Bigg[\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}1\Bigg]^{3/4}\Bigg[\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}N_{h}^{K-1}(s,a,b)\Bigg]^{1/4}\leq(SAB)^{3/4}K^{1/4}.`$ |  |

  Combining above bounds yields

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bigg(2H\eta_{0}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}+\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)+\underline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)\bigg)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{4c_{\mathrm{b}}H^{2}}{\big(N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{3/4}}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle 2HSAB\!\!+\!\!\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)\!\!+\!\!\underline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)\!\!+\!\!16c_{\mathrm{b}}(SAB)^{3/4}K^{1/4}H^{2}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}.`$ |  | (180) |
- •
  We now turn to the second term of
  ([177](#A4.E177 "In Appendix D Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
  A little algebra gives

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)\big)`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{l=1}^{K}\sum_{N=N_{h}^{l}(s_{h}^{l},a_{h}^{l},b_{h}^{l})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{l},a_{h}^{l},b_{h}^{l})}\eta_{N_{h}^{l}(s_{h}^{l},a_{h}^{l},b_{h}^{l})}^{N}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{l}(s_{h+1}^{l})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{l}(s_{h+1}^{l})\big),`$ |  | (181) |

  where the second line replaces $`k^{n}`$ (resp. $`n`$) with $`l`$
  (resp. $`N_{h}^{l}(s_{h}^{l},a_{h}^{l})`$). Combined with the property
  $`\sum_{N=n}^{\infty}\eta_{n}^{N}\leq 1+1/H`$ (see
  Lemma [1](#Thmlemma1 "Lemma 1 ‣ A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  we get

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle(\ref{equ:remove-v-star1})`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)\sum_{l=1}^{K}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{l}(s_{h+1}^{l})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{l}(s_{h+1}^{l})\big)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle=\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})\big),`$ |  | (182) |

  where the last relation replaces $`l`$ with $`k`$.
- •
  When it comes to the last term of
  ([177](#A4.E177 "In Appendix D Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  based on
  $`V_{h+1}^{\star}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)=P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}`$,
  we can derive

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}_{n}\Bigg(\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)\Bigg)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\eta^{N_{h}^{k-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}_{n}\Bigg(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)-\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)\Bigg)`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(V_{h+1}^{\star}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)\right)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\star}\right)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(V_{h+1}^{\star}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}}\right)\right)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\eta_{n}^{N_{h}^{k-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\star}\right).`$ |  | (183) |

  With
  $`\Lambda_{h}^{k}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{N=N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}^{N_{h}^{K-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\eta_{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}^{N}`$,
  there is

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle(\ref{eq:sum_VR})\!=\!`$ | $`\displaystyle\Lambda_{h}^{k}\left(\left(P_{h}^{k}\!-\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\right)\left(V_{h+1}^{\star}\!-\!\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)\!-\!\left(P_{h}^{k}\!-\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\right)\left(V_{h+1}^{\star}\!-\!\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)\right)`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle+\Lambda_{h}^{k}\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)}{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}`$ |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle-\Lambda_{h}^{k}\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)}{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}.`$ |  |

  Here, the first equality holds since
  $`V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-V^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})=P_{h}^{k^{n}}\big(V^{\star}_{h+1}-V^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\big)`$
  (in view of the definition of $`P_{h}^{k}`$ in
  ([15](#A1.E15 "In 2nd item ‣ A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))),
  the final equality can be seen via simple rearrangement of the terms,
  while in the last line we replace $`k^{n}`$ (resp. $`n`$) with $`k`$
  (resp. $`N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})`$).

Taking the above bounds together with
([177](#A4.E177 "In Appendix D Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([31](#A1.E31 "In A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we can rearrange terms to reach

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(\overline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})-\underline{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})+\zeta_{h}^{k}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})\big)+\overline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)+\underline{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+2HSAB+16c_{\mathrm{b}}(SAB)^{3/4}K^{1/4}H^{2}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\zeta_{h}^{k}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\Lambda_{h}^{k}\left(\left(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\right)\left(V_{h+1}^{\star}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)-\left(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\right)\left(V_{h+1}^{\star}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)\right)`$ |  | (184) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\Lambda_{h}^{k}\left(\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)}{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\Lambda_{h}^{k}\left(\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\underline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)}{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\right).`$ |  | (185) |

Thus far, we have established a crucial connection between
$`\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(\overline{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})\big)`$
at step $`h`$ and
$`\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})\big)`$
at step $`h+1`$. Clearly, the term
$`\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})\big)`$
can be further bounded in the same manner. As a result, by recursively
applying the above relation
([185](#A4.E185 "In Appendix D Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
over the time steps $`h=1,2,\cdots,H`$ and using the terminal condition
$`\overline{V}^{k}_{H+1}=\underline{V}^{k}_{H+1}=0`$, we can immediately
arrive at the advertised bound in
Lemma [5](#Thmlemma5 "Lemma 5 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").

## Appendix E Proof of Lemma [6](#Thmlemma6 "Lemma 6 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")

### E.1 Bounding the term $`\mathcal{D}_{1}`$

First of all, let us look at the first two terms of $`\mathcal{D}_{1}`$
in
([35a](#A1.E35.1 "In 35 ‣ Lemma 5 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Recognizing the following elementary inequality

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{H}\leq e\quad\text{for all }h=1,2,\cdots,H+1,
``` |  | (186) |

we are allowed to upper bound the first two terms in
([35a](#A1.E35.1 "In 35 ‣ Lemma 5 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
as follows:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}\left\{2HSAB+16c_{\mathrm{b}}H^{2}(SAB)^{3/4}K^{1/4}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim H^{2}SAB+H^{3}(SAB)^{3/4}K^{1/4}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}\lesssim H^{4.5}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sqrt{H^{3}SABK}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad=H^{4.5}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sqrt{H^{2}SABT},`$ |  | (187) |

where the last inequality can be shown using the AM-GM inequality as
follows:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle H^{3}(SAB)^{3/4}K^{1/4}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\Big(H^{9/4}\sqrt{SAB}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}\Big)\cdot(H^{3}SABK)^{1/4}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq H^{4.5}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sqrt{H^{3}SABK}.`$ |  |

We are now left with the last term of $`\mathcal{D}_{1}`$ in
([35a](#A1.E35.1 "In 35 ‣ Lemma 5 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Towards this, we know that $`\zeta_{h}^{k}`$ is a martingale difference
sequence (w.r.t both $`h`$ and $`k`$), and
$`\left\lvert\zeta_{h}^{k}\right\rvert\leq 4H`$. Hence by the
Azuma-Hoeffding inequality and a union bound, we have with probability
at least $`1-\delta/2`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\zeta_{h}^{k}\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}\sqrt{HK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}T\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}`$ |  | (188) |

with probability exceeding $`1-\delta`$.

Combining
([187](#A5.E187 "In E.1 Bounding the term 𝒟_1 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([188](#A5.E188 "In E.1 Bounding the term 𝒟_1 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
with the definition
([35a](#A1.E35.1 "In 35 ‣ Lemma 5 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
of $`\mathcal{D}_{1}`$ immediately leads to the claimed bound.

### E.2 Bounding the term $`\mathcal{D}_{2}`$

In view of the definition of
$`\overline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})`$
(resp.
$`\underline{B}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})`$) in
line 15
Algorithm [2](#alg2 "In 𝑄-function estimation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
we can decompose $`\mathcal{D}_{2}`$
(cf. ([35b](#A1.E35.2 "In 35 ‣ Lemma 5 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")))
as follows:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{H\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{H\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\underline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})-\big(\underline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\underline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})-\big(\underline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sqrt{\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}},`$ |  | (189) |

where the last relation holds due to
([186](#A5.E186 "In E.1 Bounding the term 𝒟_1 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and the symmetry of
$`[\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a}},\underline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a}}]`$,
$`[\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a}},\underline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a}}]`$,
$`[\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r}},\underline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r}}]`$ and
$`[\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r}},\underline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r}}]`$. In
what follows, we intend to bound these two terms separately.

##### Step 1: upper bounding the first term in ([189](#A5.E189 "In E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

Towards this, we make the observation that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}_{n}\big(\overline{V}^{k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big)^{2}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}},`$ |  | (190) |

where the second line follows from the update rule of
$`\overline{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k}`$ in
([102](#A3.E102 "In C.1.1 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Combining the relation
$`|\overline{V}^{k}_{h+1}(s_{h}^{k})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}(s_{h}^{k})|\leq 2`$
(cf. ([27](#A1.E27 "In Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")))
and the property
$`\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\eta^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}_{n}\leq 1`$
(cf. ([18](#A1.E18 "In A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")))
with
([190](#A5.E190 "In Step 1: upper bounding the first term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
yields

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{a},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{4}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}\leq 4\sqrt{SABK}.`$ |  | (191) |

Here, the last inequality holds due to the following fact:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\sum_{n=1}^{N^{K}_{h}(s,a,b)}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}}\leq 2\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\sqrt{N^{K}_{h}(s,a,b)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}1}\cdot\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}N^{K}_{h}(s,a,b)}=2\sqrt{SABK},`$ |  | (192) |

where the last line arises from Cauchy-Schwarz and the basic fact that
$`\sum_{(s,a,b)}N^{K}_{h}(s,a,b)=K`$.

##### Step 2: upper bounding the second term in ([189](#A5.E189 "In E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

Recalling the update rules of $`\overline{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k}`$ and
$`\overline{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k}`$ in
([121](#A3.E121 "In C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}}=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}R_{h}^{k}.`$ |  | (193) |

with

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
R_{h}^{k}=\sqrt{\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big)^{2}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}-\bigg(\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\bigg)^{2}}.
``` |  |

Additionally, the quantity $`R_{h}^{k}`$ defined in
([193](#A5.E193 "In Step 2: upper bounding the second term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
obeys

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle(R_{h}^{k})^{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big)^{2}-\big({V}^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big)^{2}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\big({V}^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big)^{2}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}-\Bigg(\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}{V}^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\Bigg)^{2}\leq L_{1}+L_{2},`$ |  | (194) |

which arises from the fact that
$`H\geq\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}\geq V^{\star}_{h+1}\geq 0`$
for all $`k^{n}\leq K`$, and

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle L_{1}=\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}2H\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-{V}^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big)}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})},`$ |  | (195) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle L_{2}=\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\big({V}^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big)^{2}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}-\Bigg(\frac{\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}{V}^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\Bigg)^{2}.`$ |  | (196) |

Therefore, we have

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\big)^{2}\!\!-\!\!\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\big)^{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})+V_{h+1}^{\star}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\big)\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})-V_{h+1}^{\star}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2H\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})-V_{h+1}^{\star}(s_{h+1}^{k^{n}})\big).`$ |  |

With
([194](#A5.E194 "In Step 2: upper bounding the second term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
in mind, we shall proceed to bound each term in
([194](#A5.E194 "In Step 2: upper bounding the second term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
separately.

- •
  The first term $`L_{1}`$ can be straightforwardly bounded as follows

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle L_{1}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\frac{2H}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}\Bigg(\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}V^{\prime}\mathbbm{1}\Big(V^{\prime}\leq 3\Big)+\Phi^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\Bigg)`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 6H+\frac{2H}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}\Phi^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}),`$ |  | (197) |

  where $`\Phi^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})`$ is defined as

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  \Phi^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})\coloneqq\sum_{n=1}^{N^{k}_{h}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}V^{\prime}\mathbbm{1}\Big(V^{\prime}>3\Big)
  ``` |  | (198) |

  with
  $`V^{\prime}=\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})`$.
- •
  When it comes to the second term $`L_{2}`$, we claim that

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle L_{2}\lesssim\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})+H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}},`$ |  | (199) |

  which will be justified in
  Appendix [E.2.1](#A5.SS2.SSS1 "E.2.1 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").

Plugging
([197](#A5.E197 "In 1st item ‣ Step 2: upper bounding the second term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([199](#A5.E199 "In 2nd item ‣ Step 2: upper bounding the second term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
into
([194](#A5.E194 "In Step 2: upper bounding the second term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([193](#A5.E193 "In Step 2: upper bounding the second term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
allows one to demonstrate that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})-\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{1}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}\sqrt{H\!\!+\!\!\frac{H\Phi^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\!\!+\!\!\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})\!\!+\!\!H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Bigg(\sqrt{\frac{H}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}\!\!+\!\!\frac{\sqrt{H\Phi^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\!\!+\!\!\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}\!\!+\!\!\frac{H\log^{1/2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{\big(N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{3/4}}\Bigg).`$ |  | (200) |

Combined with
([192](#A5.E192 "In Step 1: upper bounding the first term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([179](#A4.E179 "In 1st item ‣ Appendix D Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
there is

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle(\ref{equ:bound of var1})`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{HSABK}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{H\Phi^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}+H(SAB)^{3/4}\left(K\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}\right)^{1/4}.`$ |  | (201) |

##### Step 3: putting together the preceding results.

Finally, the above results in
([191](#A5.E191 "In Step 1: upper bounding the first term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([201](#A5.E201 "In Step 2: upper bounding the second term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
taken collectively with
([189](#A5.E189 "In E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
lead to

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{3}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\!\!+\!\!\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sqrt{\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\overline{\psi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\!\!-\!\!\big(\overline{\phi}^{\mathrm{r},k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})\big)^{2}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b^{k}_{h})}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{3}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{2}(SAB)^{3/4}K^{1/4}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sqrt{\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}+\sqrt{H\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\Phi^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}.`$ |  |

Furthermore, we claimed two inequalities as follows:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3.5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta},`$ |  | (202) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\Phi^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim H^{7/2}SAB\log^{3/2}\frac{SABT}{\delta},`$ |  | (203) |

whose proofs are postponed to
Appendix [E.2.2](#A5.SS2.SSS2 "E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and
Appendix [E.2.3](#A5.SS2.SSS3 "E.2.3 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
respectively. Thus, there is

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle{\lesssim}\sqrt{H^{3}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{2}(SAB)^{3/4}K^{1/4}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}+H^{4}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle{\lesssim}\sqrt{H^{3}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{4}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sqrt{H^{2}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{4}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}.`$ |  |

where the second line above is valid since

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle H^{2}(SAB)^{3/4}K^{1/4}\log^{5/4}\frac{SABT}{\delta}\!\!=\!\!\bigg(H^{5/4}(SAB)^{1/2}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}\bigg)\!\!\cdot\!\!\bigg(H^{3}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}\bigg)^{1/4}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad\lesssim H^{2.5}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sqrt{H^{3}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\quad=H^{2.5}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sqrt{H^{2}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}`$ |  |

due to the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality. This concludes the proof of the
advertised upper bound on $`\mathcal{D}_{2}`$.

#### E.2.1 Proof of the inequality ([199](#A5.E199 "In 2nd item ‣ Step 2: upper bounding the second term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))

Akin to the proof of $`W_{4,1}`$ in
([125](#A3.Ex8b "In Step 1: bounding 𝑊_{4,1}. ‣ C.1.2 Proof of inequality () ‣ C.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
let

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle F_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq({V}^{\star}_{h+1})^{2}\qquad\text{and}\qquad G_{h}^{k}(s,a,b,N)\coloneqq\frac{1}{N}.`$ |  |

By observing and setting

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\|F_{h+1}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq H^{2}\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{f}},\qquad C_{\mathrm{g}}\coloneqq\frac{1}{N},`$ |  |

we can apply
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
to yield

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\big(V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\big)^{2}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})^{2}\bigg|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V^{\star}_{h+1}\big)^{2}\bigg|\lesssim H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}}`$ |  |

with probability at least $`1-\delta`$. Similarly, by applying the
trivial bound $`\|V_{h+1}^{\star}\|_{\infty}\leq H`$ and
Lemma [7](#Thmlemma7 "Lemma 7 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
we can obtain

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\!\!-\!\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V^{\star}_{h+1}\bigg|=\bigg|\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\big(P^{k^{n}}_{h}\!\!-\!\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)V^{\star}_{h+1}\bigg|\lesssim H\sqrt{\frac{\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}}`$ |  |

with probability at least $`1-\delta`$.

Recalling from
([16](#A1.E16 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
the definition

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})=P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})^{2}-\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V^{\star}_{h+1}\big)^{2},
``` |  |

we can use the preceding two bounds and the triangle inequality to show
that: with probability at least $`1-\delta`$, there is

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})^{2}-\Bigg(\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\Bigg)^{2}-\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})\Bigg|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})^{2}\!\!-\!\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})^{2}\Bigg|\!\!-\!\!\Bigg|\bigg(\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\bigg)^{2}\!\!-\!\!(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V^{\star}_{h+1})^{2}\Bigg|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\!\lesssim\!\Bigg|\!\frac{1}{N_{h}^{k}}\!\sum_{n=1}^{N_{h}^{k}}\!V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\!\!-\!\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V^{\star}_{h+1}\!\Bigg|\Bigg|\!\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\!\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}\!V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\!\!+\!\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V^{\star}_{h+1}\!\Bigg|\!\!+\!\!H^{2}\!\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}\!\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}}.`$ |  |

And thus, with the fact that $`\|V_{h+1}^{\star}\|_{\infty}\leq H`$, we
get

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\Bigg|\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})^{2}-\Bigg(\frac{1}{{N_{h}^{k}}}\sum_{n=1}^{{N_{h}^{k}}}V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})\Bigg)^{2}-\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})\Bigg|\lesssim H^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}{{N_{h}^{k}}}}.
``` |  |

#### E.2.2 Proof of the inequality ([202](#A5.E202 "In Step 3: putting together the preceding results. ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))

To begin with, we make the observation that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k})}}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\sum_{n=1}^{N^{K}_{h}(s,a,b)}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Var}_{h,s,a,b}(V^{\star}_{h+1})}{n}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\sqrt{N^{K}_{h}(s,a,b)\mathsf{Var}_{h,s,a,b}(V^{\star}_{h+1})},`$ |  |

which relies on the fact that
$`\sum_{n=1}^{N}1/{\sqrt{n}}\leq 2\sqrt{N}`$. It then follows that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k})}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\sqrt{N^{K}_{h}(s,a,b)\mathsf{Var}_{h,s,a,b}(V^{\star}_{h+1})}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}1}\cdot\sqrt{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}N^{K}_{h}(s,a,b)\mathsf{Var}_{h,s,a,b}(V^{\star}_{h+1})}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=2\sqrt{HSAB}\sqrt{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})}\,,`$ |  | (204) |

where the second inequality invokes the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.

The rest of the proof is then dedicated to bounding
([204](#A5.E204 "In E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Towards this end, we first decompose

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k}}\big(V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1}\big)+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left|\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})-\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1})\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle{\lesssim}HT+H^{3}\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left|\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})-\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b_{h}^{k}}(V^{\pi^{k}}_{h+1})\right|,`$ |  | (205) |

where the last inequality follows from ([Jin et al., 2018b](#bib.bib11),
Lemma C.5) for a formal proof. The second term on the right-hand side of
([205](#A5.E205 "In E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
can be bounded as follows

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left|\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V_{h+1}^{\star})-\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V_{h+1}^{\star})^{2}\!-\!\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)^{2}\!-\!P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}})^{2}\!+\!\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)^{2}\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\bigg\{\left|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\Big(\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}+V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)\Big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\Big|\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)^{2}-\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)^{2}\Big|\bigg\}.`$ |  | (206) |

Before next proof, we first state that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\Big(\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}+V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)\Big)\right|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)\right|\Big(\big\|V_{h+1}^{\star}\big\|_{\infty}+\big\|V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big\|_{\infty}\big)\leq 2H\left|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)\right|,`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\bigg|\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)^{2}-\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)^{2}\bigg|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\Big|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)\Big|\cdot\Big|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}+V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)\Big|\leq 2H\left|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)\right|.`$ |  |

And thus, there is

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle(\ref{equ:var-vsatr-22})`$ | $`\displaystyle{\leq}4H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}-V_{h+1}^{\pi^{k}}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{i})}{\leq}4H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=4H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left\{\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})+\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}-P_{h}^{k}\big)\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}\big)\right\}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 4H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(\delta_{h+1}^{k}+\chi_{h+1}^{k}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{(\mathrm{ii})}{\lesssim}H^{2}\sqrt{T\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+\sqrt{H^{7}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{4}SAB`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\asymp\sqrt{H^{7}SABT\log\frac{SAT}{\delta}}+H^{4}SAB,`$ |  | (207) |

where we define

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\delta_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k}),\qquad\chi_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq\big(P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}-P_{h}^{k}\big)\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}\big).`$ |  | (208) |

We shall take a moment to explain how we derive
([207](#A5.E207 "In E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
The inequality (ii) is valid since
$`\underline{V}_{h+1}\leq V^{\star}\leq\overline{V}_{h+1}`$ and
$`\underline{V}_{h+1}\leq V^{\pi}\leq\overline{V}_{h+1}`$; and (iii)
results from the following two bounds:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\delta_{h+1}^{k}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(eH^{2}SAB+\sqrt{H^{3}T\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+10c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{eH^{3}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{5}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3}SAB,`$ |  | (209a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\chi_{h+1}^{k}`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim H\sqrt{T\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}},`$ |  | (209b) |

where the first inequality in
([209a](#A5.E209.1 "In 209 ‣ E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
comes from
([144](#A3.E144 "In C.2.1 Proof of Lemma ‣ C.2 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
with $`w_{k}=1`$ and $`C\leq K`$, and
([209b](#A5.E209.2 "In 209 ‣ E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
comes from the Azuma-Hoeffding inequality and a union bound.

As a consequence, substituting
([205](#A5.E205 "In E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([207](#A5.E207 "In E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
into
([204](#A5.E204 "In E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
we reach

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sqrt{\frac{\mathsf{Var}_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}(V_{h+1}^{\star})}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{HSAB}\sqrt{HT+\sqrt{H^{7}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{4}SAB}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}SABT}+H^{9/4}(SAB)^{3/4}\Big(T\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}\Big)^{1/4}+H^{2.5}SAB`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sqrt{H^{2}SABT}+\Big(H^{2}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}\Big)^{1/4}\big(H^{3.5}SAB\big)^{1/2}+H^{2.5}SAB`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3.5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta},`$ |  |

where we have applied the basic inequality $`2ab\leq a^{2}+b^{2}`$ for
any $`a,b\geq 0`$.

#### E.2.3 Proof of the inequality ([203](#A5.E203 "In Step 3: putting together the preceding results. ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))

First, it is observed that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\Phi_{h}^{k}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}=\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\sum_{n=1}^{N^{K}_{h}(s,a,b)}\frac{\sqrt{\Phi_{h}^{k^{n}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b)}}{n}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\sqrt{\Phi_{h}^{N_{h}^{K}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b)}\log T\leq\sqrt{SAB\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\Phi_{h}^{N_{h}^{K}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b)}\log T.`$ |  | (210) |

Here, the first inequality holds by the monotonicity property of
$`\Phi_{h}^{k}(s_{h},a_{h})`$ with respect to $`k`$ (see its definition
in
([198](#A5.E198 "In 1st item ‣ Step 2: upper bounding the second term in (). ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")))
due to the same property of $`V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}`$, while the second
inequality comes from Cauchy-Schwarz.

To continue, with
$`V^{\prime}=\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{n}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})-V^{\star}_{h+1}(s^{k^{n}}_{h+1})`$,
note that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\Phi_{h}^{N_{h}^{K}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b)}=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Big(V^{\prime}\Big)\mathbbm{1}\Big(V^{\prime}>3\Big)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})+2-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})\Big)\mathbbm{1}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})+2-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})>3\Big)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})+2-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})\Big)\mathbbm{1}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})>1\Big)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sqrt{\sum_{k=1}^{K}3\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})\Big)\mathbbm{1}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})>1\Big)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{H}\sqrt{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}3\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})\Big)\mathbbm{1}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})>1\Big)},`$ |  | (211) |

where the first inequality follows from
Lemma [4](#Thmlemma4 "Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
(cf. ([27](#A1.E27 "In Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")))
and
Lemma [2](#Thmlemma2 "Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
(so that
$`\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-V_{h+1}^{\star}(s_{h+1}^{k})\leq\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})+2-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})`$),
the penultimate inequality holds since
$`1\leq\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})`$
when
$`\mathbbm{1}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})>1\Big)\neq 0`$,
and the last inequality is a consequence of the Cauchy-Schwarz
inequality.

Combining the above relation with
([171](#A3.E171 "In Case 2. ‣ C.3.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ C.3 Proof of Lemma ‣ Appendix C Proof of key lemmas in Section ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and applying the triangle inequality, we can demonstrate that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sqrt{\sum_{(s,a,b)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times\mathcal{B}}\Phi_{h}^{N_{h}^{K}(s,a,b)}(s,a,b)}\lesssim\sqrt{H^{7}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}},`$ |  |

where the inequality follows directly from
([28](#A1.E28 "In Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
Substitution into
([210](#A5.E210 "In E.2.3 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
gives

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\sqrt{\Phi_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}}{N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}`$ | $`\displaystyle\!\!\lesssim\!\!\left(\sqrt{SAB}\log T\right)\cdot\sqrt{H^{7}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\!\!\asymp\!\!H^{7/2}SAB\log^{3/2}\frac{SABT}{\delta},`$ |  |

thus concluding the proof.

### E.3 Bounding the term $`\mathcal{D}_{3}`$

First of all, there is

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\lambda^{k}_{h}\coloneqq\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}~\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\eta^{n}_{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h}\leq\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{H}\leq e,
``` |  | (212) |

where the first inequality in
([212](#A5.E212 "In E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
follows from the property $`\sum_{N=n}^{\infty}\eta_{n}^{N}\leq 1+1/H`$
in
Lemma [1](#Thmlemma1 "Lemma 1 ‣ A.2 Preliminaries: basic properties about learning rates ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and the last inequality in
([212](#A5.E212 "In E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
results from
([186](#A5.E186 "In E.1 Bounding the term 𝒟_1 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Then we could decompose the expression of $`\mathcal{D}_{3}`$ in
([35c](#A1.E35.3 "In 35 ‣ Lemma 5 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
as follows

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{3}:=\mathcal{D}_{3,1}+\mathcal{D}_{3,2}-\mathcal{D}_{3,3}-\mathcal{D}_{3,4}`$ |  |

with

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{3,1}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda^{k}_{h}\big(P^{k}_{h}-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\big)\big(V^{\star}_{h+1}-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}\big)`$ |  | (213) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{3,2}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda^{k}_{h}\frac{\sum_{i\leq N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{i}}_{h+1})-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}\big)}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}`$ |  | (214) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{3,3}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda^{k}_{h}\big(P^{k}_{h}-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\big)\big(V^{\star}_{h+1}-\underline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}\big)`$ |  | (215) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{3,4}`$ | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda^{k}_{h}\frac{\sum_{i\leq N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\big(\underline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{i}}_{h+1})-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\underline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}\big)}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}.`$ |  | (216) |

In the sequel, we shall control each of these two terms separately.

##### Step 1: upper bounding $`\mathcal{D}_{3,1}`$ and $`\mathcal{D}_{3,3}`$.

We plan to control this term by means of
Lemma [8](#Thmlemma8 "Lemma 8 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
For notational simplicity, let us define

|     |                                                          |     |
|-----|----------------------------------------------------------|-----|
|     | $`\displaystyle N(s,a,b,h)\coloneqq N_{h}^{K-1}(s,a,b)`$ |     |

and set

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle F_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}-V^{\star}_{h+1}\quad\text{and}\quad G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k})\coloneqq\lambda_{h}^{k}=\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h},h)}\eta^{n}_{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}.`$ |  |

Given the fact that
$`\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}(s),V^{\star}_{h+1}(s)\in[0,H]`$ and
the condition
([212](#A5.E212 "In E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
it is readily seen that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big\|F_{h+1}^{k}\big\|_{\infty}\leq H\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{f}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\big|G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big|\leq e\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{g}}.`$ |  |

Apply
Lemma [8](#Thmlemma8 "Lemma 8 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
to yield, with probability at least $`1-\delta/2`$, there is

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{D}_{3,1}\right|=`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{h}^{k}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{f}}HSAB\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[P_{h}^{k}F_{h+1}^{k}\right]\log\frac{K}{\delta}}+C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}HSAB\log\frac{K}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{k,h-1}\left[P_{h}^{k}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right]\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+H^{2}SAB\log\frac{T}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\asymp`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\bigg\{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\bigg\}\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+H^{2}SAB\log\frac{T}{\delta}`$ |  | (217) |

with
$`X_{k,h}=\lambda_{h}^{k}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\big)`$.

It then comes down to controlling the sum
$`\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)`$.
Towards this end, we first single out the following useful fact: with
probability at least $`1-{\delta}/{4}`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}P^{k}_{h}\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\leq}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}P^{k}_{h}\big(\overline{V}^{k}_{h+1}+2-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2HK\!+\!\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Big(\overline{V}^{k}_{h+1}(s_{h+1}^{k})-V_{h+1}^{\star}(s_{h+1}^{k})\Big)\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\lesssim}H^{3}\sqrt{SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\!+\!H^{3}SAB\!+\!HK,`$ |  | (218) |

where (i) holds according to
([27](#A1.E27 "In Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
and (ii) is valid since

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-V_{h+1}^{\star}(s_{h+1}^{k})\Big)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\Big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})-\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}(s_{h+1}^{k})\Big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim H^{3}\sqrt{SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3}SAB,`$ |  |

where the first inequality follows since
$`V_{h+1}^{\star}\geq\underline{V}_{h+1}^{k}`$, and the second
inequality comes from
([209a](#A5.E209.1 "In 209 ‣ E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Additionally, invoking Freedman’s inequality (see
Lemma [8](#Thmlemma8 "Lemma 8 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
with $`c_{h}=1`$ and
$`\widetilde{W}_{h}^{i}=\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-V_{h+1}^{\star}`$
(so that $`0\leq\widetilde{W}_{h}^{i}(s)\leq H`$) directly leads to

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right|`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{TH^{2}\log\frac{1}{\delta}}+H\log\frac{1}{\delta}\asymp\sqrt{H^{3}K\log\frac{1}{\delta}}`$ |  |

with probability at least $`1-\delta/4`$, which taken collectively with
([218](#A5.E218 "In Step 1: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,1} and 𝒟_{3,3}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
reveals that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}P_{s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}P_{h}^{k}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)+\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},h}\big)\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle H^{3}\sqrt{SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3}SAB+HK`$ |  | (219) |

with probability at least $`1-\delta/2`$. Substitution into
([217](#A5.E217 "In Step 1: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,1} and 𝒟_{3,3}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
then gives

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{D}_{3,1}\right|=`$ | $`\displaystyle\Big|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{h}^{k}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\star}-\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\big)\Big|`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+H^{2}SAB\log\frac{T}{\delta}`$ |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\left(H^{3}\sqrt{SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3}SAB+HK\right)\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+H^{2}SAB\log\frac{T}{\delta}.`$ |  |

According to the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, there is

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sqrt{H^{6}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}=\sqrt{H^{5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{HK}\lesssim H^{5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}+HK,
``` |  |

and then we can further obtain

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{D}_{3,1}\right|\asymp`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\left(H^{5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}+H^{3}SAB+HK\right)\log\frac{T}{\delta}}+H^{2}SAB\log\frac{T}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{H^{3}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3.5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{H^{2}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3.5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}.`$ |  | (220) |

Similarly,
$`\left|\mathcal{D}_{3,3}\right|\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}SABT\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3.5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$.

##### Step 2: upper bounding $`\mathcal{D}_{3,2}`$ and $`\mathcal{D}_{3,4}`$.

According to the monotonicity property
$`\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}\geq\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}_{h+1}\geq\cdots\geq\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h+1}`$,
we make the following observation:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{3,2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\frac{\lambda^{k}_{h}}{N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\sum_{i\leq N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}_{h+1}(s^{k^{i}}_{h+1})-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h+1}\big)`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle=\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\frac{\lambda^{k}_{h}}{n}\Big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}(s^{k}_{h+1})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h+1}(s^{k}_{h+1})+\big(P^{k}_{h}-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\big)\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h+1}\Big).`$ |  |

And with the facts that
$`\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\frac{1}{n}\leq\log T`$
and $`\lambda_{h}^{k}\leq e`$
(cf. ([212](#A5.E212 "In E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))),
there is

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{3,2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq(e\log T)\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}(s^{k}_{h+1})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h+1}(s^{k}_{h+1})\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\frac{\lambda^{k}_{h}}{n}\big(P^{k}_{h}-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\big)V^{\star}_{h+1}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\frac{\lambda^{k}_{h}}{n}\big(P^{k}_{h}-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h+1}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big).`$ |  | (221) |

In what follows, we shall control the three terms in
([221](#A5.E221 "In Step 2: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,2} and 𝒟_{3,4}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
separately.

- •
  The first term in
  ([221](#A5.E221 "In Step 2: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,2} and 𝒟_{3,4}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
  can be controlled by
  Lemma [4](#Thmlemma4 "Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
  (cf. ([28](#A1.E28 "In Lemma 4 ‣ Properties of the reference 𝑉̄_ℎ^{R,𝑘} and 𝑉̱_ℎ^{R,𝑘} ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")))
  as follows:

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  \sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h+1}(s^{k}_{h+1})-\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h+1}(s^{k}_{h+1})\big)\lesssim H^{6}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}
  ``` |  | (222) |

  with probability at least $`1-{\delta}/{3}`$.
- •
  To control the second term in
  ([221](#A5.E221 "In Step 2: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,2} and 𝒟_{3,4}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  we abuse the notation by setting

  |     |                                                          |     |
  |-----|----------------------------------------------------------|-----|
  |     | $`\displaystyle N(s,a,b,h)\coloneqq N_{h}^{K-1}(s,a,b)`$ |     |

  and

  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle F_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq V^{\star}_{h+1},\qquad\text{and}\qquad G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\coloneqq\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h)}\frac{\lambda^{k}_{h}}{n},`$ |  |

  which clearly satisfy

  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\|F_{h+1}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq H\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{f}}\quad\text{and}\quad\big|G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})\big|\leq e\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{i}_{h},a^{i}_{h},b^{i}_{h})}^{N(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h)}\frac{1}{n}\leq e\log T\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{g}}.`$ |  |

  Here, we have made use of the properties
  $`\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\frac{1}{n}\leq\log T`$
  and $`\lambda_{h}^{k}\leq e`$
  (cf. ([212](#A5.E212 "In E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))).
  With these in place, applying
````
