````math
  Lemma [8](#Thmlemma8 "Lemma 8 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
  reveals that, with probability at least $`1-{\delta}/{3}`$,

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\frac{\lambda^{k}_{h}}{n}\big(P^{k}_{h}-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\big)V^{\star}_{h+1}\right|=\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}X_{k,h}\right|`$ |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}HSAB\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{i,h-1}\left[\big|(P_{h}^{i}-P_{h,s_{h}^{i},a_{h}^{i}})W_{h+1}^{k}\big|^{2}\right]\log\frac{T}{\delta}}\!+\!C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}HSAB\log\frac{T}{\delta}`$ |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle{\asymp}`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\mathsf{Var}_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}(V^{\star}_{h+1})\cdot HSAB\log^{3}\frac{T}{\delta}}+H^{2}SAB\log^{2}\frac{T}{\delta},`$ |  |

  where
  $`X_{k,h}=\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\frac{\lambda^{k}_{h}}{n}\big(P^{k}_{h}-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\big)V^{\star}_{h+1}`$
  and the last line comes from the definition in
  ([16](#A1.E16 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
  Therefore, we have

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\frac{\lambda^{k}_{h}}{n}\big(P^{k}_{h}-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\big)V^{\star}_{h+1}\right|`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle{\lesssim}`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{HSAB\big(HT+\sqrt{H^{7}SABT}+H^{4}SAB\big)\log^{4}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{2}SAB\log^{2}\frac{T}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{HSAB\big(HT+H^{6}SAB+H^{4}SAB\big)\log^{4}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{2}SAB\log^{2}\frac{T}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
  |  | $`\displaystyle{\lesssim}`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{H^{2}SABT\log^{4}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3.5}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta},`$ |  | (223) |

  where the second line holds due to
  ([205](#A5.E205 "In E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
  and
  ([207](#A5.E207 "In E.2.2 Proof of the inequality () ‣ E.2 Bounding the term 𝒟_2 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  and the last line is valid since

  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  HT+\sqrt{H^{7}SABT}=HT+\sqrt{H^{6}SAB}\cdot\sqrt{HT}\lesssim HT+H^{6}SAB
  ``` |  |

  due to the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality.
- •
  Turning attention the third term of
  ([221](#A5.E221 "In Step 2: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,2} and 𝒟_{3,4}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  we need to properly cope with the dependency between $`P_{h}^{k}`$ and
  $`V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},K}`$. Towards this, we shall resort to the
  standard epsilon-net argument (see, e.g., ([Tao, 2012](#bib.bib13))),
  which will be presented in
  Appendix [E.3.1](#A5.SS3.SSS1 "E.3.1 Proof of () ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
  The final bound reads like

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},K}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
  |  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad\lesssim H^{3.5}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sqrt{H^{3}SABK\log^{3}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}.`$ |  | (224) |
- •
  Combining
  ([222](#A5.E222 "In 1st item ‣ Step 2: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,2} and 𝒟_{3,4}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  ([223](#A5.E223 "In 2nd item ‣ Step 2: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,2} and 𝒟_{3,4}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  and
  ([224](#A5.E224 "In 3rd item ‣ Step 2: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,2} and 𝒟_{3,4}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
  with
  ([221](#A5.E221 "In Step 2: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,2} and 𝒟_{3,4}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
  we can use the union bound to demonstrate that

  |  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|----|
  |  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{3,2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{3,2}\bigg\{H^{6}SAB\log^{3}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sqrt{H^{2}SABT\log^{4}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\bigg\}`$ |  | (225) |

  with probability at least $`1-\delta`$, where $`C_{3,2}>0`$ is some
  constant.

Similarly,
$`\mathcal{D}_{3,4}\leq C_{3,4}\bigg\{H^{6}SAB\log^{3}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sqrt{H^{2}SABT\log^{4}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\bigg\}`$
with probability at least $`1-\delta`$, where $`C_{3,4}>0`$ is some
constant.

##### Step 3: final bound of $`\mathcal{D}_{3}`$.

Putting the above results
([220](#A5.E220 "In Step 1: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,1} and 𝒟_{3,3}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([225](#A5.E225 "In 4th item ‣ Step 2: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,2} and 𝒟_{3,4}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
together, we immediately arrive at

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{D}_{3}\leq\big|\mathcal{D}_{3,1}\big|\!+\!\mathcal{D}_{3,2}\!+\!\big|\mathcal{D}_{3,3}\big|\!+\!\mathcal{D}_{3,4}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{\mathrm{r},3}\bigg\{H^{6}SAB\log^{3}\frac{SABT}{\delta}\!+\!\sqrt{H^{2}SABT\log^{4}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\bigg\}`$ |  | (226) |

with probability at least $`1-2\delta`$, where $`C_{\mathrm{r},3}>0`$ is
some constant. This immediately concludes the proof.

#### E.3.1 Proof of ([224](#A5.E224 "In 3rd item ‣ Step 2: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,2} and 𝒟_{3,4}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))

##### Step 1: concentration bounds for a fixed group of vectors.

Consider a fixed group of vectors
$`\{V^{\mathrm{d}}_{h+1}\in\mathbb{R}^{S}\mid 1\leq h\leq H\}`$ obeying
the following properties:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h+1}^{\star}\leq V^{\mathrm{d}}_{h+1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq H\qquad\text{ for }1\leq h\leq H.`$ |  | (227) |

We intend to control the following sum

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{d}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big).
``` |  |

To do so, we shall resort to
Lemma [8](#Thmlemma8 "Lemma 8 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
For the moment, let us take $`N(s,a,h)\coloneqq N_{h}^{K-1}(s,a,b)`$ and

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle F_{h+1}^{k}\coloneqq V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{d}}-V^{\star}_{h+1},\qquad\mathrm{and}\qquad G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k})\coloneqq\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{i}_{h},a^{i}_{h},b^{i}_{h})}^{N(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h)}\frac{\lambda^{k}_{h}}{n}.`$ |  |

It is easy to see that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\big|G_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k})\big|\leq e\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k},h)}\frac{1}{n}\leq e\log T\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{g}}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\|F_{h+1}^{k}\|_{\infty}\leq H\eqqcolon C_{\mathrm{f}},`$ |  |

which hold due to the facts
$`\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\frac{1}{n}\leq\log T`$
and $`\lambda_{h}^{k}\leq e`$
(cf. ([212](#A5.E212 "In E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")))
as well as the property that
$`V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{d}}(s),V^{\star}_{h+1}(s)\in[0,H]`$. Thus, invoking
Lemma [8](#Thmlemma8 "Lemma 8 ‣ B.2 Application of Freedman’s inequality ‣ Appendix B Freedman’s inequality ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
yields

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{d}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right|=\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}X_{k,h}\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{C_{\mathrm{g}}^{2}C_{\mathrm{f}}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{i=1}^{K}\mathbb{E}_{i,h-1}\left[P_{h}^{i}F_{h+1}^{k}\right]\log\frac{K^{HSAB}}{\delta_{0}}}+C_{\mathrm{g}}C_{\mathrm{f}}\log\frac{K^{HSAB}}{\delta_{0}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{i=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\left(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{d}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\right)\big(\log^{2}T\big)\log\frac{K^{HSAB}}{\delta_{0}}}+H\big(\log T\big)\log\frac{K^{HSAB}}{\delta_{0}}`$ |  | (228) |

with probability at least $`1-\delta_{0}`$, where the choice of
$`\delta_{0}`$ will be revealed momentarily.

##### Step 2: constructing and controlling an epsilon net.

Our argument in Step 1 is only applicable to a fixed group of vectors.
The next step is then to construct an epsilon net that allows one to
cover the set of interest. Specifically, let us construct an epsilon net
$`\mathcal{N}_{h+1,\alpha}`$ (the value of $`\alpha`$ will be specified
shortly) for each $`h\in[H]`$ such that:

- a)
  For any $`V_{h+1}\in[0,H]^{S}`$, one can find a point
  $`V^{\mathrm{net}}_{h+1}\in\mathcal{N}_{h+1,\alpha}`$ obeying

  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  0\leq V_{h+1}(s)-V^{\mathrm{net}}_{h+1}(s)\leq\alpha\qquad\text{for all }s\in\mathcal{S};
  ``` |  |
- b)
  Its cardinality obeys

  |  |  |  |  |
  |----|----|----|----|
  |  |
  ``` math
  \big|\mathcal{N}_{h+1,\alpha}\big|\leq\Big(\frac{H}{\alpha}\Big)^{S}.
  ``` |  | (229) |

Clearly, this also means that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\big|\mathcal{N}_{2,\alpha}\times\mathcal{N}_{3,\alpha}\times\cdots\times\mathcal{N}_{H+1,\alpha}\big|\leq\Big(\frac{H}{\alpha}\Big)^{SH}.
``` |  |

Set $`\delta_{0}=\frac{1}{6}\delta/\big(\frac{H}{\alpha}\big)^{SH}`$.
Taking
([228](#A5.E228 "In Step 1: concentration bounds for a fixed group of vectors. ‣ E.3.1 Proof of () ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
together the union bound implies that: with probability at least
$`1-\delta_{0}\big(\frac{H}{\alpha}\big)^{SH}=1-\delta/6`$, one has

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{net}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{i=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\left(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{net}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\right)\big(\log^{2}T\big)\log\frac{K^{HSAB}}{\delta_{0}}}+H(\log T)\log\frac{K^{HSAB}}{\delta_{0}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{i=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\left(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{net}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\right)\big(\log^{2}T\big)\log\frac{SAT}{\delta\alpha}}+H^{2}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta\alpha}`$ |  | (230) |

simultaneously for all $`\{V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{net}}\mid 1\leq h\leq H\}`$
obeying $`V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{d}}\in\mathcal{N}_{h+1,\alpha}`$
($`h\in[H]`$).

##### Step 3: obtaining uniform bounds.

We are now positioned to establish a uniform bound over the entire set
of interest. Consider an arbitrary group of vectors
$`\{V^{\mathrm{u}}_{h+1}\in\mathbb{R}^{S}\mid 1\leq h\leq H\}`$ obeying
([227](#A5.E227 "In Step 1: concentration bounds for a fixed group of vectors. ‣ E.3.1 Proof of () ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
By construction, one can find a group of points
$`\big\{V^{\mathrm{net}}_{h+1}\in\mathcal{N}_{h+1,\alpha}\mid h\in[H]\big\}`$
such that

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle 0\leq V^{\mathrm{u}}_{h+1}(s)-V^{\mathrm{net}}_{h+1}(s)\leq\alpha\qquad\text{for all }(h,s)\in\mathcal{S}\times[H].`$ |  | (231) |

It is readily seen that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{u}}-V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{net}}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\Big(\big\|P_{h}^{k}\big\|_{1}+\big\|P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big\|_{1}\Big)\big\|V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{u}}-V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{net}}\big\|_{\infty}\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq 2eK\alpha\log T,`$ |  | (232) |

where the last inequality follows from
$`\sum_{n=N^{i}_{h}(s^{i}_{h},a^{i}_{h},b^{i}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{i}_{h},a^{i}_{h},b^{i}_{h})}\frac{1}{n}\leq\log T`$
and $`\lambda_{h}^{k}\leq e`$
(cf. ([212](#A5.E212 "In E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))).
Consequently, by taking $`\alpha=1/(SABT)`$, we can deduce that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{u}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\leq`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{net}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left|\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{u}}-V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{net}}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{net}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right|+HK\alpha\log T`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{i=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\left(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{net}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\right)\big(\log^{2}T\big)\log\frac{SABT}{\delta\alpha}}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+H^{2}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta\alpha}+HK\alpha\log T`$ |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\asymp`$ | $`\displaystyle\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{i=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\left(V_{h+1}^{\mathrm{u}}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\right)\big(\log^{2}T\big)\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\!\!+\!\!H^{2}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta},`$ |  | (233) |

where the last line holds due to the condition
([231](#A5.E231 "In Step 3: obtaining uniform bounds. ‣ E.3.1 Proof of () ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and our choice of $`\alpha`$. To summarize, with probability exceeding
$`1-\delta/6`$, the property
([233](#A5.E233 "In Step 3: obtaining uniform bounds. ‣ E.3.1 Proof of () ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
holds simultaneously for all
$`\{V^{\mathrm{u}}_{h+1}\in\mathbb{R}^{S}\mid 1\leq h\leq H\}`$ obeying
([227](#A5.E227 "In Step 1: concentration bounds for a fixed group of vectors. ‣ E.3.1 Proof of () ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).

##### Step 4: controlling the original term of interest.

With the above union bound in hand, we are ready to control the original
term of interest

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N^{k}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}^{N^{K-1}_{h}(s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h})}\frac{\lambda^{k}_{h}}{n}\big(P^{k}_{h}-P_{h,s^{k}_{h},a^{k}_{h},b^{k}_{h}}\big)\big(\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h+1}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big).`$ |  | (234) |

To begin with, it can be easily verified using
([26](#A1.E26 "In Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle V_{h+1}^{\star}\leq\overline{V}^{\mathrm{R},K}_{h+1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq H\qquad\text{ for all }1\leq h\leq H.`$ |  | (235) |

Moreover, we make the observation that

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},K}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)`$ | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(i)}}{\leq}\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overset{\mathrm{(ii)}}{\leq}\sqrt{H^{6}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3}SAB+HK`$ |  | (236) |

with probability exceeding $`1-\delta/6`$, where (i) holds because
$`\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R}}`$ is monotonically non-increasing (in
view of the monotonicity of $`\overline{V}_{h}(s)`$ in
([23](#A1.E23 "In Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and the update rule in line 17 of
Algorithm [3](#alg3 "In A.1 Additional notation ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")),
and (ii) follows from
([219](#A5.E219 "In Step 1: upper bounding 𝒟_{3,1} and 𝒟_{3,3}. ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")).
Substitution into
([233](#A5.E233 "In Step 3: obtaining uniform bounds. ‣ E.3.1 Proof of () ‣ E.3 Bounding the term 𝒟_3 ‣ Appendix E Proof of Lemma ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
yields

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},K}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right|`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{i=1}^{K}P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\left(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},K}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\right)\big(\log^{2}T\big)\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{2}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\left\{\sqrt{H^{6}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{3}SAB+HK\right\}\big(\log^{2}T\big)\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}`$ |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\qquad+H^{2}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}.`$ |  |

According to the fact that

|  |  |  |
|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\sqrt{H^{6}SABK\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}=\sqrt{H^{5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}}\sqrt{HK}\lesssim H^{5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}+HK,
``` |  |

we have

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\left|\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\sum_{n=N_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}^{N_{h}^{K-1}(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k})}\frac{\lambda_{h}^{k}}{n}\big(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}}\big)\big(\overline{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},K}-V_{h+1}^{\star}\big)\right|`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}SAB\left\{H^{5}SAB\log\frac{SABT}{\delta}+H^{3}SAB+HK\right\}\log^{3}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}+H^{2}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lesssim H^{3.5}SAB\log^{2}\frac{SABT}{\delta}+\sqrt{H^{3}SABK\log^{3}\frac{SABT}{\delta}}.`$ |  | (237) |

## Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games

In this section, we extend ME-Nash-QL to the setting of multiplayer
general-sum Markov games and present the corresponding theoretical
guarantees.

### F.1 Problem formulation

A general-sum Markov game (general-sum MG) is a tuple
$`\mathcal{M}(\mathcal{S},\{\mathcal{A}_{i}\}_{i=1}^{m},H,\{P_{h}\}_{h=1}^{H},\{r_{i}\}_{i=1}^{m})`$
with $`m`$ players, where $`\mathcal{S}`$ denotes the state space and
$`H`$ is the horizon length. We have $`m`$ different action spaces,
where $`\mathcal{A}_{i}`$ is the action space for the $`i^{\text{th}}`$
player and $`|\mathcal{A}_{i}|=A_{i}`$. We let
$`\mathcal{A}=\mathcal{A}_{1}\times\cdots\times\mathcal{A}_{m}`$ denote
the joint action space, and let
$`\bm{a}:=(a_{1},\cdots,a_{m})\in\mathcal{A}`$ denote the (tuple of)
joint actions by all $`m`$ players. $`\{P_{h}\}_{h\in[H]}`$ is a
collection of transition matrices, so that $`P_{h}(\cdot|s,\bm{a})`$
gives the distribution of the next state if actions $`\bm{a}`$ are taken
at state $`s`$ at step $`h`$, and $`r_{i}=\{r_{h,i}\}_{h\in[H]}`$ is a
collection of reward functions for the $`i^{\text{th}}`$ player, so that
$`r_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})`$ gives the reward received by the $`i^{\text{th}}`$
player if actions $`\bm{a}`$ are taken at state $`s`$ at step $`h`$.

The policy of the $`i^{\text{th}}`$ player is denoted as
$`\pi_{i}:=\big\{\pi_{h,i}:\mathcal{S}\rightarrow\Delta_{\mathcal{A}_{i}}\big\}_{h\in[H]}`$.
We denote the product policy of all players as
$`\pi:=\pi_{1}\times\cdots\times\pi_{M}`$, and denote the policy of all
players except the $`i^{\text{th}}`$ player as $`\pi_{-i}`$. We define
$`V^{\pi}_{h,i}(s)`$ as the expected cumulative reward that will be
received by the $`i^{\text{th}}`$ player if starting at state $`s`$ at
step $`h`$ and all players follow policy $`\pi`$. For any strategy
$`\pi_{-i}`$, there also exists a *best response* of the
$`i^{\text{th}}`$ player, which is a policy $`\mu^{\dagger}(\pi_{-i})`$
satisfying
$`V_{h,i}^{\mu^{\dagger}(\pi_{-i}),\pi_{-i}}(s)=\sup_{\pi_{i}}V_{h,i}^{\pi_{i},\pi_{-i}}(s)`$
for any $`(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times[H]`$. For convenience, we denote
$`V_{h,i}^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}}:=V_{h,i}^{\mu^{\dagger}(\pi_{-i}),\pi_{-i}}`$.
The $`Q`$-functions of the best response can be defined similarly.

In general, there are three versions of equilibrium for general-sum MGs:
Nash equilibrium (NE), correlated equilibrium (CE), and coarse
correlated equilibrium (CCE), all being standard solution notions in
games ([Nisan et al., 2007](#bib.bib45)). These three notions coincide
in two-player zero-sum games, but are not equivalent to each other in
multi-player general-sum games; any one of them could be desired
depending on the application at hand. In this section, we first consider
CCE and then extend to NE and CE in the final of
Section [F.2](#A6.SS2 "F.2 Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").

The CCE is a relaxed version of Nash equilibrium in which we consider
general correlated policies instead of product policies.

###### Definition 4 (CCE in general-sum MGs)

A (correlated) policy
$`\pi:=\{\pi_{h}(s)\in\Delta_{\mathcal{A}}:\ (h,s)\in[H]\times\mathcal{S}\}`$
is a CCE if
$`\max_{i\in[m]}V^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}}_{h,i}(s)\leq V^{\pi}_{h,i}(s)`$ for
all $`(s,h)\in\mathcal{S}\times[H]`$.

Compared with a Nash equilibrium, a CCE is not necessarily a product
policy, that is, we may not have
$`\pi_{h}(s)\in\Delta_{\mathcal{A}_{1}}\times\cdots\times\Delta_{\mathcal{A}_{m}}`$.
Similarly, we also define $`\epsilon`$-approximate CCE and CCE-regret as
below.

###### Definition 5 ($`\epsilon`$-approximate CCE in general-sum MGs)

A policy
$`\pi:=\{\pi_{h}(s)\in\Delta_{\mathcal{A}}:\ (h,s)\in[H]\times\mathcal{S}\}`$
is an $`\epsilon`$-approximate CCE if
$`\frac{1}{K}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{i\in[m]}{(V_{1,i}^{{\dagger},\pi_{-i}}-V_{1,i}^{\pi})}(s_{1}^{k})\leq\epsilon`$.

Parameter: some universal constant $`c_{\mathrm{b}}>0`$ and probability
of failure $`\delta\in(0,1)`$

1 Initialize: $`\overline{Q}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s,\bm{a})`$, $`\leftarrow H`$;
$`\underline{Q}_{h,i}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s,\bm{a})`$$`\leftarrow 0`$;
$`\overline{V}_{h,i}(s)`$,
$`\overline{V}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)`$$`\leftarrow H`$;
$`\underline{V}_{h,i}(s)`$,
$`\underline{V}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s)`$$`\leftarrow 0`$;
$`N_{h}(s,\bm{a})\leftarrow 0`$;
$`\overline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\underline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\overline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\underline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\overline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\underline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\overline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\underline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\overline{\varphi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\underline{\varphi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\overline{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\underline{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s,\bm{a})\leftarrow 0`$; and
$`{u}_{\mathrm{r},i}(s)=\mathsf{True}`$ for all
$`(s,\bm{a},h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times[H]`$.

2 for *Episode $`k=1,\dots,K`$* do

    3 Set initial state $`s_{1}\leftarrow s_{1}^{k}`$.

    4 for *Step $`h=1,\dots,H`$* do

       5 Take action $`\bm{a}_{h}\sim\pi_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})`$, and draw
$`s_{h+1}\sim P_{h}(\cdot\mid s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})`$.

       6
$`N_{h}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})\leftarrow N_{h}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})+1`$;$`\quad n\leftarrow N_{h}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})`$;$`\quad\eta_{n}\leftarrow\frac{H+1}{H+n}`$.

       7 for *player $`i=1,2,\cdots,m`$* do

          8
$`\left[Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB}},Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}\right](s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})\leftarrow\mathrm{update\mathchar 45\relax q}\left(\right)`$.

          9
$`\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})\leftarrow\mathrm{update\mathchar 45\relax ur}\left(\right)`$.

          10
$`\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})\leftarrow\mathrm{update\mathchar 45\relax lr}\left(\right)`$.

          11
$`\overline{Q}_{h,i}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})\leftarrow\min{\{\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}),Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB}}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}),\overline{Q}_{h,i}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})\}}`$.

          12
$`\underline{Q}_{h,i}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})\leftarrow\max{\{\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}),Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}),\underline{Q}_{h,i}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})\}}`$.

          13 if
*$`\overline{Q}_{h,i}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})=\min{\{\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}),Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB}}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})\}}`$
and
$`\underline{Q}_{h,i}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})=\max{\{\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}),Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h})\}}`$*
then

             14
$`\pi_{h}(\cdot|s_{h})\leftarrow\mathrm{CCE}(\overline{Q}_{h,1}(s_{h},\cdot),\cdots,\underline{Q}_{h,m}(s_{h},\cdot)).`$

          15
$`\overline{V}_{h,i}(s_{h})\leftarrow\min\{(\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h}}\overline{Q}_{h,i})(s_{h}),\overline{V}_{h,i}(s_{h})\}`$;

          16
$`\underline{V}_{h,i}(s_{h})\leftarrow\max\{(\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h}}\underline{Q}_{h,i})(s_{h}),\underline{V}_{h,i}(s_{h})\}`$.

          17 if
*$`\overline{V}_{h,i}(s_{h})-\underline{V}_{h,i}(s_{h})>1`$* then

             18
$`\overline{V}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{h})\leftarrow\overline{V}_{h,i}(s_{h})`$;$`\quad\underline{V}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{h})\leftarrow\underline{V}_{h,i}(s_{h})`$.

          19 else if *$`{u}_{\mathrm{r},i}(s_{h})=\mathsf{True}`$* then

             20
$`\overline{V}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{h})\leftarrow\overline{V}_{h,i}(s_{h})`$;
$`\quad\underline{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R}}(s_{h})\leftarrow\underline{V}_{h,i}(s_{h})`$;
$`\quad{u}_{\mathrm{r},i}(s_{h})=\mathsf{False}`$.

21 Output: $`\{\pi_{h}\}_{h=1}^{H}`$.

Algorithm 4 Multi-player Memory-Efficient Nash Q-Learning
(Multi-ME-Nash-QL)

###### Definition 6 (CCE-regret in general-sum MGs)

Let policy $`\pi^{k}`$ denote the (correlated) policy deployed by the
algorithm in the $`k^{\text{th}}`$ episode. After a total of $`K`$
episodes, the regret is defined as

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\operatorname{Regret}(K)=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{i\in[m]}(V_{1,i}^{{\dagger},\pi_{-i}^{k}}-V_{1,i}^{\pi^{k}})(s_{1}^{k}).
``` |  | (238) |

In addition, for general-sum MGs, we have
$`\{\rm NE\}\subseteq\{\rm CE\}\subseteq\{\rm CCE\}`$, so that they form
a nested set of notions of equilibria ([Nisan et al.,
2007](#bib.bib45)). Finally, since a Nash equilibrium always exists, so
a CE and CCE equilibrium always exist.

Function
*update-q$`\left(\left[Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB}},Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}\right](s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}),\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,i},\underline{V}_{h+1,i}\right]\left(s_{h+1}\right)\right)`$*
:

    1
$`Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\!\leftarrow\!\left(1\!-\!\eta_{n}\right)Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\!+\!\eta_{n}\left(r_{h,i}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\!+\!\overline{V}_{h+1,i}\left(s_{h+1}\right)\!+\!c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}H^{3}\log\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}\right)`$;

    2
$`Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\!\leftarrow\!\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)Q_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\!+\!\eta_{n}\left(r_{h,i}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\!+\!\underline{V}_{h+1,i}\left(s_{h+1}\right)\!-\!c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}H^{3}\log\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}\right)`$.

3 Function
*update-ur$`\left(\left[\overline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}},\overline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}},\overline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}},\overline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}},\overline{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}},\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\right]\!\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\!,\!\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}},\overline{V}_{h+1,i}\right]\!(s_{h+1})\right)`$*
:

    4
$`\left[\overline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right),\overline{b}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\right]\leftarrow\mathrm{update\mathchar 45\relax q\mathchar 45\relax bonus}\left(\left[\overline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}},\overline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}},\overline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}},\overline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}},\overline{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\right]\!\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\!,\!\left[\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}},\overline{V}_{h+1,i}\right]\!(s_{h+1})\right)`$;

    5
$`\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\leftarrow\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)+\eta_{n}\left(r_{h,i}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)+\overline{V}_{h+1,i}\left(s_{h+1}\right)-\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h+1}\right)+\overline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)+\overline{b}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\right)`$.

6 Function
*update-lr$`\left(\left[\underline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}},\underline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}},\underline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}},\underline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}},\underline{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}},\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\right]\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right),\left[\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}},\underline{V}_{h+1,i}\right](s_{h+1})\right)`$*
:

    7
$`\left[\underline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right),\underline{b}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\right]\leftarrow\mathrm{update\mathchar 45\relax q\mathchar 45\relax bonus}\left(\left[\underline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}},\underline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}},\underline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}},\underline{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}},\underline{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\right]\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right),\left[\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}},\underline{V}_{h+1,i}\right](s_{h+1})\right)`$;

    8
$`\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\leftarrow\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)+\eta_{n}\left(r_{h,i}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)+\underline{V}_{h+1,i}\left(s_{h+1}\right)-\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h+1}\right)+\underline{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)-\underline{b}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\right)`$.

9 Function
*update-q-bonus$`\left(\left[{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}},{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}},{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}},{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}},{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\right]\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right),\left[{V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}},{V}_{h+1,i}\right](s_{h+1})\right)`$*
:

    10
$`{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\leftarrow\left(1-\frac{1}{n}\right){\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)+\frac{1}{n}{V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h+1}\right)`$;

    11
$`{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\leftarrow\left(1-\frac{1}{n}\right){\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\left({V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h+1}\right)\right)^{2}`$;

    12
$`{\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\leftarrow\left(1-\eta_{n}\right){\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)+\eta_{n}\left({V}_{h+1,i}\left(s_{h+1}\right)-{V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h+1}\right)\right)`$;

    13
$`{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\leftarrow\left(1-\eta_{n}\right){\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)+\eta_{n}\left({V}_{h+1,i}\left(s_{h+1}\right)-{V}_{h+1,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h+1}\right)\right)^{2}`$;

    14
$`{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{temp}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\leftarrow c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}{n}}\sqrt{{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)-\left({\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{r}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\right)^{2}}+c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}{n}}\sqrt{H}\sqrt{{\psi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)-\left({\phi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{a}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\right)^{2}}`$;

    15
$`{\varphi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\leftarrow{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{temp}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)-{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)`$;

    16
$`{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)\leftarrow{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{temp}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)`$;

    17
$`{b}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\leftarrow{B}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)+\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\frac{{\varphi}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R}}\left(s_{h},\bm{a}_{h}\right)}{\eta_{n}}+c_{\mathrm{b}}\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}{n^{3/4}}`$.

Algorithm 5 Auxiliary functions of Multi-ME-Nash-QL

### F.2 Multi-ME-Nash-QL

Here we present the Multi-ME-Nash-QL algorithm in
Algorithm [4](#alg4 "In F.1 Problem formulation ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
which is an extension of
Algorithm [1](#alg1 "In 𝑄-function estimation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
for multi-player general-sum Markov games.

Remarkably, the CCE operation in line 15 in Algorithm
[4](#alg4 "In F.1 Problem formulation ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
could be replaced by NE or CE operation which finds the NE or CE for
*one-step* games. When using NE operation, the worst-case computational
complexity will be PPAD-hard. When using CE or CCE, it can be solved in
polynomial time using linear programming. However, the policies found
are not guaranteed to be a product policy. We remark that in
Algorithm [1](#alg1 "In 𝑄-function estimation ‣ 3.1 Algorithm description ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
we used the CCE subroutine for finding Nash in two-player zero-sum
games, which seemingly contrasts the principle of using the NE
subroutine for finding the Nash equilibrium, but nevertheless works as
the Nash equilibrium and CCE are equivalent in zero-sum games.

### F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL

In this section, we prove
Theorem [3](#Thmtheorem3 "Theorem 3 ‣ Sample complexity and burn-in cost ‣ 3.2 Main results ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
Similar to
Lemma [2](#Thmlemma2 "Lemma 2 ‣ Properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate ‣ A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
in
Appendix [A.3.1](#A1.SS3.SSS1 "A.3.1 Key properties and auxiliary sequences ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
we can obtain the properties of the Q-estimate and V-estimate, as
asserted by the following lemma.

###### Lemma 10

Consider any $`\delta\in(0,1)`$. Suppose that $`c_{\mathrm{b}}>0`$ is
some sufficiently large constant. Then with probability at least
$`1-\delta`$,

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s,\bm{a})\!\geq\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{k}(s,\bm{a})\geq Q_{h,i}^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}^{k}}(s,\bm{a}),\quad\overline{V}_{h,i}^{k}(s)\geq V_{h,i}^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}^{k}}(s),`$ |  | (239) |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s,\bm{a})\!\leq\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{k}(s,\bm{a})\leq Q_{h,i}^{\pi^{k}}(s,\bm{a}),\quad\underline{V}_{h,i}^{k}(s)\leq V_{h,i}^{\pi^{k}}(s)`$ |  | (240) |

hold simultaneously for all
$`(s,\bm{a},h)\in\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{A}\times[H]`$.

##### Step 1: regret decomposition.

Firstly, we can apply
Lemma [10](#Thmlemma10 "Lemma 10 ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
to reformulate
([238](#A6.E238 "In Definition 6 (CCE-regret in general-sum MGs) ‣ F.1 Problem formulation ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
as

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\operatorname{Regret}(K)=\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{i}\left(V_{1,i}^{\dagger,\pi_{-i}^{k}}-V_{1,i}^{\pi^{k}}\right)\left(s_{1}^{k}\right)\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{i}\left(\overline{V}_{1,i}^{k}-\underline{V}_{1,i}^{k}\right)\left(s_{1}^{k}\right).`$ |  | (241) |

According to lines 13-14 in
Algorithm [4](#alg4 "In F.1 Problem formulation ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
we obtain

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s,\bm{a})\geq\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{k}(s,\bm{a}),\quad\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s,\bm{a})\leq\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{k}(s,\bm{a}),`$ |  | (242) |
|  | $`\displaystyle{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB},k}(s,\bm{a})\geq\overline{Q}_{h,i}^{k}(s,\bm{a}),\quad{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB},k}(s,\bm{a})\leq\underline{Q}_{h,i}^{k}(s,\bm{a}).`$ |  | (243) |

Based on this relation, we notice the following propagation by
lines 18-21 in
Algorithm [4](#alg4 "In F.1 Problem formulation ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and update rules in
Algorithm [5](#alg5 "In F.1 Problem formulation ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
with
$`c_{b}^{\mathrm{ULCB}}=c_{b}\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}H^{3}\log\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}`$:

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\left\{\begin{aligned} &\left(\overline{Q}^{k}_{h,i}-\underline{Q}^{k}_{h,i}\right)(s,\bm{a})\leq\left({Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB},k}-{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB},k}\right)(s,\bm{a}),\\
&\left({Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB},k}-{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB},k}\right)(s,\bm{a})=\!\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\left({Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{UCB},k}-{Q}_{h,i}^{\mathrm{LCB},k}\right)(s,\bm{a})\\
&\qquad\qquad+\eta_{n}\left(\left(\overline{V}_{h+1,i}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h+1,i}^{k}\right)\left(s_{h+1}^{k}\right)+2c_{b}^{\mathrm{ULCB}}\right),\\
&\left(\overline{V}_{h,i}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h,i}^{k}\right)(s)=\left[\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h}}\left(\overline{Q}^{k}_{h,i}-\underline{Q}^{k}_{h,i}\right)\right](s).\end{aligned}\right.
``` |  | (244) |

We can define $`\hat{V}^{k}_{h}(s)`$, $`\check{V}^{k}_{h}(s)`$,
$`\hat{Q}^{k}_{h}(s,\bm{a})`$, $`{\check{Q}_{h}^{k}}(s,\bm{a})`$,
$`\hat{Q}^{\mathrm{UCB},k}_{h}(s,\bm{a})`$ and
$`{\check{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{LCB},k}}`$ recursively by
$`\hat{V}^{k}_{H+1}(s)=0`$ and $`\check{V}^{k}_{H+1}(s)=0`$, and there
is

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\left\{\begin{aligned} &\left(\hat{Q}^{k}_{h}-\check{Q}^{k}_{h}\right)(s,\bm{a})=\left(\hat{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},k}-\check{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{LCB},k}\right)(s,\bm{a}),\\
&\left(\hat{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},k+1}-\check{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{LCB},k+1}\right)(s,\bm{a})=\!\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\left(\hat{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{UCB},k}-\check{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{LCB},k}\right)(s,\bm{a})\\
&\qquad\qquad+\eta_{n}\left(\left(\hat{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\check{V}_{h+1}^{k}\right)\left(s_{h+1}^{k}\right)+2c_{b}^{\mathrm{ULCB}}\right),\\
&\left(\hat{V}_{h}^{k}-\check{V}_{h}^{k}\right)(s)=\left[\mathbb{D}_{\pi_{h}}\left(\hat{Q}^{k}_{h}-\check{Q}^{k}_{h}\right)\right](s).\end{aligned}\right.
``` |  | (245) |

Similarly, we also have the following definitions.

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\left\{\begin{aligned} &\hat{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\left(1-\frac{1}{n}\right)\hat{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\hat{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s\right),\\
&\check{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\left(1-\frac{1}{n}\right)\check{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\check{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s\right),\\
&\hat{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\left(1-\frac{1}{n}\right)\hat{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\left(\hat{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s\right)\right)^{2},\\
&\check{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\left(1-\frac{1}{n}\right)\check{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)+\frac{1}{n}\left(\check{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s\right)\right)^{2},\\
&\hat{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\hat{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)+\eta_{n}\left(\hat{V}_{h+1}^{k}\left(s\right)-\hat{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s\right)\right),\\
&\check{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\check{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)+\eta_{n}\left(\check{V}_{h+1}^{k}\left(s\right)-\check{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s\right)\right),\\
&\hat{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\hat{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)+\eta_{n}\left(\hat{V}_{h+1}^{k}\left(s\right)-\hat{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s\right)\right)^{2},\\
&\check{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\check{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)+\eta_{n}\left(\check{V}_{h+1}^{k}\left(s\right)-\check{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s\right)\right)^{2},\\
&\widetilde{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{temp},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=c_{\mathrm{b}}\sqrt{\frac{\log^{2}\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}{n}}\times\Bigg(\\
&\qquad\qquad\left(\sqrt{\hat{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)-\left(\hat{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)\right)^{2}}+\sqrt{\check{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)-\left(\check{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)\right)^{2}}\right)\\
&\qquad+\sqrt{H}\left(\sqrt{\hat{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)-\left(\hat{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)\right)^{2}}+\sqrt{\check{\psi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)-\left(\check{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{a},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)\right)^{2}}\right)\Bigg);\\
&\widetilde{\varphi}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\widetilde{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{temp},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)-\widetilde{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s,\bm{a}\right);\\
&\widetilde{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\widetilde{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{temp},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right);\\
&\widetilde{b}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}=\widetilde{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)+\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\frac{\widetilde{\varphi}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)}{\eta_{n}}+2c_{\mathrm{b}}\frac{H^{2}\log^{2}\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}{n^{3/4}},\\
&\widetilde{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)=\left(1-\eta_{n}\right)\widetilde{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s,\bm{a}\right)\\
&\qquad+\eta_{n}\left(\widetilde{b}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k+1}+\left(\hat{V}_{h+1}^{k}-\check{V}_{h+1}^{k}\right)\left(s\right)-\left(\hat{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-\check{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)\left(s\right)+\left(\hat{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k+1}-\check{\phi}_{h}^{\mathrm{r},k+1}\right)\left(s,\bm{a}\right)\right),\end{aligned}\right.
``` |  | (246) |

where $`\hat{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}`$ and
$`\check{V}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}`$ are associated with
$`\hat{V}^{k}_{h}(s)`$ and $`\check{V}^{k}_{h}(s)`$ and updated similar
to lines 18-21 in
Algorithm [4](#alg4 "In F.1 Problem formulation ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
Then we can prove inductively that for any $`k`$, $`h`$, $`s`$ and
$`{\bm{a}}`$,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  |
``` math
\max_{i}(\overline{V}_{h,i}^{k}-\underline{V}_{h,i}^{k})(s)\leq\hat{V}^{k}_{h}(s)-\check{V}^{k}_{h}(s).
``` |  | (247) |

Therefore, we only need to bound
$`\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\hat{V}^{k}_{1}(s)-\check{V}^{k}_{1}(s)\right)`$,
that is,

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\operatorname{Regret}(K)\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\max_{i}\left(\overline{V}_{1,i}^{k}-\underline{V}_{1,i}^{k}\right)\left(s_{1}^{k}\right)\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\left(\hat{V}^{k}_{1}(s_{1}^{k})-\check{V}^{k}_{1}(s_{1}^{k})\right).`$ |  | (248) |

To continue, we intend to examine
$`\left(\hat{V}_{h}^{k}-\check{V}_{h}^{k}\right)(s_{h}^{k})`$ across all
time steps $`1\leq h\leq H`$, which admits the following decomposition:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\hat{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})-\check{V}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k})`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h}^{k}}(\hat{Q}_{h}^{k}-\check{Q}_{h}^{k})(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a})=\hat{Q}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k})-\check{Q}_{h}^{k}(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k})+\zeta_{h}^{k}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq\widetilde{Q}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k})+\zeta_{h}^{k},`$ |  | (249) |

where

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\zeta_{h}^{k}:=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{a}\sim\pi_{h}^{k}}(\hat{Q}_{h}^{k}-\check{Q}_{h}^{k})(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a})-(\hat{Q}_{h}^{k}-\check{Q}_{h}^{k})(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k}).`$ |  | (250) |

Summing
([248](#A6.E248 "In Step 1: regret decomposition. ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
and
([249](#A6.E249 "In Step 1: regret decomposition. ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"))
over $`1\leq k\leq K`$, we reach at

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\operatorname{Regret}(K)\leq\sum_{k=1}^{K}\widetilde{Q}_{1}^{\mathrm{R},k}(s_{1}^{k},\bm{a}_{1}^{k})+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\zeta_{1}^{k}.`$ |  | (251) |

##### Step 2: managing regret by recursion.

The regret can be further manipulated by leveraging the update rule of
$`\hat{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h}`$ and $`\check{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h}`$,
which is similar to that of $`\overline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h,i}`$ and
$`\underline{Q}^{\mathrm{R},k}_{h,i}`$. This leads to a key
decomposition as summarized as follows. The proof of Lemma
[11](#Thmlemma11 "Lemma 11 ‣ Step 2: managing regret by recursion. ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
is similar to that of Lemma
[5](#Thmlemma5 "Lemma 5 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning"),
and is omitted here.

###### Lemma 11

Fix $`\delta\in(0,1)`$. Suppose that $`c_{\mathrm{b}}>0`$ is a
sufficiently large constant. Then with probability at least
$`1-\delta`$, one has

|  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{K}\hat{V}_{1}^{k}(s_{1}^{k})-\check{V}_{1}^{k}(s_{1}^{k})\leq\mathcal{H}_{1}+\mathcal{H}_{2}+\mathcal{H}_{3},`$ |  | (252) |

where

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  |  |  |  |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1}=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}\left(2HS\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}+16c_{\mathrm{b}}(S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i})^{3/4}K^{1/4}H^{2}\log\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}+\sum_{k=1}^{K}\zeta_{h}^{k}\right),`$ |  | (253a) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{2}=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}\left(\sum_{k=1}^{K}\widetilde{B}_{h}^{\mathrm{R},k}\left(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k}\right)\right),`$ |  | (253b) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{3}=`$ | $`\displaystyle\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{h}^{k}\left(P_{h}^{k}-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k}}\right)\left(\check{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}-\hat{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle+\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{h}^{k}\frac{\sum\nolimits_{i=1}^{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\hat{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k}}\hat{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)}{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k}\right)}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle-\sum_{h=1}^{H}\sum_{k=1}^{K}\lambda_{h}^{k}\left(\frac{\sum_{i=1}^{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k}\right)}\left(\check{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k^{i}}\left(s_{h+1}^{k^{i}}\right)-P_{h,s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k}}\check{V}_{h+1}^{\mathrm{R},k}\right)}{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},\bm{a}_{h}^{k}\right)}\right)`$ |  | (253c) |

with

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\lambda_{h}^{k}=\left(1+\frac{1}{H}\right)^{h-1}\sum\nolimits_{N=N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}^{N_{h}^{K-1}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}\eta_{N_{h}^{k}\left(s_{h}^{k},a_{h}^{k},b_{h}^{k}\right)}^{N}.`$ |  | (254) |

##### Step 3: controlling the terms in Step 2 separately.

Each of the terms in Step 2 can be well controlled. To derive the above
bounds, the main strategy is to apply the Bernstein-type concentration
inequalities carefully, and to upper bound the sum of variance. We
provide the bounds for these terms as
Lemma [12](#Thmlemma12 "Lemma 12 ‣ Step 3: controlling the terms in Step 2 separately. ‣ F.3 Analysis of Multi-ME-Nash-QL ‣ Appendix F Multiplayer General-sum Markov Games ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning").
The proof is similar to that of
Lemma [6](#Thmlemma6 "Lemma 6 ‣ A.3.2 Main summary of steps in Section ‣ A.3 Key lemmas ‣ Appendix A Additional notation and key lemmas ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
and is omitted here.

###### Lemma 12

With any $`\delta\in(0,1)`$, the following upper bounds hold with
probability at least $`1-\delta`$:

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{1}`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T\log\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}+H^{4.5}S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}\log^{2}\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta},`$ |  | (255) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{2}`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T\log\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}+H^{4}S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}\log^{2}\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta},`$ |  | (256) |
|  | $`\displaystyle\mathcal{H}_{3}`$ | $`\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{H^{2}S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T\log^{4}\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}}+H^{6}S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}\log^{3}\frac{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T}{\delta}.`$ |  | (257) |

##### Step 4: putting all this together.

We now establish our main result. Taking the bounds in Step 3 together
with Step 2, we see that with probability at least $`1-\delta`$ and a
constant $`C_{0}>0`$, one has

|  |  |  |  |  |
|----|----|----|----|----|
|  | $`\displaystyle\operatorname{Regret}(K)`$ | $`\displaystyle\leq\mathcal{H}_{1}+\mathcal{H}_{2}+\mathcal{H}_{3}`$ |  |  |
|  |  | $`\displaystyle\leq C_{0}\left(\sqrt{H^{2}S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T\log^{4}{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T/\delta}}+H^{6}S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}\log^{3}{S\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}T/\delta}\right).`$ |  | (258) |

Theorem [3](#Thmtheorem3 "Theorem 3 ‣ Sample complexity and burn-in cost ‣ 3.2 Main results ‣ 3 Algorithm and theoretical guarantees ‣ Provable Memory Efficient Self-Play Algorithm for Model-free Reinforcement Learning")
is proved under sample complexity with $`\varepsilon`$ average regret
(i.e., $`\frac{1}{K}\operatorname{Regret}(K)\leq\varepsilon`$). Notably,
the sample complexity is proportional to $`\prod_{i=1}^{m}A_{i}`$, which
increases exponentially as the number of players increases.
````
